## 阶段 C：取数装配与止损模板（T-6 ~ T-8）

引擎要的是「给一份数，判一次」，而**取数**与**判定**混在一起时，除权日那种错只有接上真实网络才看得见。所以 T-6 把全部 IO 关进一个文件，T-7 起的模板是纯函数，测试手搓 `Snapshot`。

### T-6：`services/rules/snapshot.py`——唯一取数处

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/__init__.py`
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/snapshot.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_snapshot.py`

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_snapshot.py`

```python
"""一趟求值的两个半边：`expand`（这条规则覆盖哪些对象）与 `read`（那个对象此刻的数）。

这里断言的全是「屏幕上那条规则到底读了谁」：`watchlist` 展开成零个对象、`position` 用了
别人的账户、`thesis` 取到另一条论——三种都不会报错，只会让一条看起来开着的规则永远不响。

世界由 `support_valuation.make_container` 铺：A/601088 有 300 天日线、两期年报、逐日估值，
时钟冻在 `DAYS[-1]`。用例不联网（stub adapter），所以这里的数是可以逐字断言的。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest
import support_valuation

from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import RuleData, Target
from sams.storage.repos.rules import RuleRepo

CODE = support_valuation.CODE  # "601088"
AS_OF = support_valuation.DAYS[-1]


@pytest.fixture
def data(db, settings):
    """从容器字段直接装配：构造参数与 `bootstrap.py` 里那些服务是同一批对象。

    故意不在这里重新 `XxxRepo(db)` 一遍——那会造出第二个仓库实例，而用例要验的正是
    「引擎读到的数与屏幕上那个数是同一个」。
    """
    container = support_valuation.make_container(db, settings)
    return RuleData(
        valuation=container.valuation,
        analysis=container.analysis,
        bars=container.bars,
        symbols=container.symbols,
        accounts=container.sim_accounts,
        ledger=container.sim_ledger,
        theses=container.theses_store,
        watchlists=container.watchlists,
        settings=settings,
    )


def make_rule(db, **over) -> str:
    args = {
        "user_id": "u1",
        "kind": "stop.fixed",
        "name": "跌破 10 元",
        "target_type": "symbol",
        "target_ref": f"A/{CODE}",
        "params": {"price": 10.0},
    }
    args.update(over)
    return RuleRepo(db).create(**args)


# --- 展开 --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
```



```python
def test_a_symbol_rule_expands_to_exactly_one_target(db, data):
    rule = make_rule(db)
    assert data.expand(rule) == [Target("u1", "A", CODE, None, None)]


def test_a_watchlist_rule_expands_over_the_owners_list(db, data):
    from sams.storage.repos.watchlists import WatchlistRepo

    WatchlistRepo(db).add("u1", "A", CODE)
    WatchlistRepo(db).add("u1", "A", "600000")
    rule = make_rule(
        db,
        kind="price.cross_below",
        target_type="watchlist",
        target_ref="*",
        params={"price": 1.0},
    )

    got = sorted((t.market, t.code) for t in data.expand(rule))
    assert got == [("A", "600000"), ("A", CODE)]


def test_a_position_rule_expands_over_that_account_only(db, data):
    """§1 那张表的第三行在展开这一半：A 账户的规则不许把 B 账户的持仓也收进来。"""
    from sams.storage.repos.ledger import SimAccountRepo, SimOrderRepo

    accounts = SimAccountRepo(db)
    first = accounts.open("u1", "甲券商", market_scope=("A",))
    second = accounts.open("u1", "乙券商", market_scope=("A",))
    orders = SimOrderRepo(db)
    orders.record_fill(
        user_id="u1",
        account_id=first.id,
        market="A",
        code=CODE,
        side="buy",
        qty=100.0,
        price=10.0,
        fee=0.0,
        currency="CNY",
        ts=support_valuation.make_container.__globals__["datetime_now"](),  # 见 Step 3
    )
    rule = make_rule(
        db,
        kind="stop.pct",
        target_type="position",
        target_ref="*",
        params={"pct": 0.08},
        account_id=first.id,
    )

    got = data.expand(rule)
    assert [(t.account_id, t.code) for t in got] == [(first.id, CODE)]
    assert second.id not in [t.account_id for t in got]
```

**这一枚里那句 `ts=` 要照抄 `tests/services/test_ledger_service.py` 或 `tests/storage/test_ledger_repo.py` 里已有的写法**：`record_fill` 的参数名与必填项以那个文件为准（这份计划写于实现之前，签名以代码为是）。若那个仓库没有公开的 `record_fill`，就直接用 `container.ledger.buy(...)` 走一遍服务层——多走一步服务，但用的是已经绿着的那条路。剩下的展开用例：

```python
def test_a_thesis_rule_expands_to_that_thesis_symbol(db, data):
    from sams.storage.repos.theses import ThesisRepo

    thesis = ThesisRepo(db).create("u1", "A", "600000", "value", actor_id="u1", now=AS_OF)
    rule = make_rule(
        db,
        kind="thesis.odds_below",
        target_type="thesis",
        target_ref=thesis.id,
        thesis_id=thesis.id,
    )

    assert data.expand(rule) == [Target("u1", "A", "600000", None, thesis.id)]


def test_an_unsupported_target_type_is_refused_not_expanded_empty(db, data):
    """`portfolio` 进不了库（`_PORTFOLIO_KINDS` 为空），所以这里是第二条闸。

    返回空列表在这里是最坏的错法：屏幕上那条规则显示「启用」，而它谁也没看。
    """
    rule = make_rule(db)
    broken = make_rule(
        db, kind="price.cross_below", target_type="symbol", target_ref=f"A/{CODE}"
    )
    object.__setattr__(broken, "target_type", "portfolio")  # 绕过入库校验，只测 expand

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="组合"):
        data.expand(broken)
    assert data.expand(rule)  # 正常那条照常展开，证明上面那一枚没有污染夹具
```

读取用例：

```python
def test_read_carries_the_price_and_which_day_it_belongs_to(data):
    """现价与「它是哪天的」必须同时出门（§10.2 的新鲜度、§1 的那条谎都靠它）。"""
    snap = data.read(Target("u1", "A", CODE, None, None), as_of=AS_OF)

    assert snap.price is not None
    assert snap.price_on is not None
    assert snap.currency == "CNY"
    assert snap.report_currency == "CNY"
    assert snap.name  # 读不到名字时至少是代码本身，屏幕上不能是空的


def test_read_gives_the_folded_technical_lines(data):
    """三条线都必须是**现价口径**：它们在后复权序列上算，不折就与现价不可比（§10.2 除权那条）。"""
    snap = data.read(Target("u1", "A", CODE, None, None), as_of=AS_OF)

    assert snap.weekly_ma20 is not None
    assert snap.daily_ma60 is not None
    assert snap.atr is not None
    # 折回之后必须与现价同量级：差一个 100 倍就是漏折（样本里现价与 MA 同数量级）
    assert 0.2 <= snap.daily_ma60 / snap.price <= 5.0


def test_read_does_not_invent_a_stop_line(db, data):
    """没有论、没有持仓时：线是 None，不是 0。

    `0` 在阈值语言里是一个能触发止损的值，「读不到」必须写成 None。
    """
    snap = data.read(Target("u1", "A", "600000", None, None), as_of=AS_OF)

    assert snap.stop_line is None
    assert snap.avg_cost is None
    assert snap.qty is None


def test_a_thesis_line_wins_over_the_synthesized_one(db, data):
    """止损线优先用论上那一列（用户确认过的数），没有才退回本次合成（见 `_stop_line`）。"""
    from sams.storage.repos.theses import ThesisRepo

    theses = ThesisRepo(db)
    thesis = theses.create("u1", "A", CODE, "value", actor_id="u1", now=AS_OF)
    theses.set_levels(
        "u1", thesis.id, actor_id="u1", now=AS_OF, stop_price=9.99, target_price=30.0,
        reward_risk=2.0
    )

    snap = data.read(Target("u1", "A", CODE, None, thesis.id), as_of=AS_OF)
    assert snap.stop_line == 9.99


def test_day_open_and_prev_close_come_from_the_daily_bars(db, data):
    """`urgent` 与 `cross_*` 靠这两个数：库里只有日线，所以它们来自 `BarRepo`（§3 的裁剪）。"""
    from sams.storage.repos.bars import BarRepo

    frame = BarRepo(db).load("A", [CODE], start=AS_OF, end=AS_OF)
    snap = data.read(Target("u1", "A", CODE, None, None), as_of=AS_OF)

    if frame.height == 0:
        pytest.skip("样本库在这天没有 K：开盘价本来就该是 None")
    assert snap.day_open == float(frame["open"][0])
    assert snap.prev_close is not None
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_snapshot.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/snapshot.py`

```python
"""规则引擎唯一的取数处（spec §10「输入」那一列）。

拆成两个动作：`expand` 回答「这条规则覆盖哪些对象」，`read` 回答「那个对象此刻是什么数」。
分开的理由是冷却：去重键里有 `account_id`，所以展开必须带着账户出门。

这一层是全套规则里**唯一**允许碰 IO 的地方。模板因此是纯函数（T-7 起），除权日那种错
在手搓的 `Snapshot` 上就能复现，不必等网络。

不认识的 target_type 一律抛，不返回空列表：空列表在屏幕上长成「这条规则没触发」，
而它真实的意思是「这条规则谁也没看」。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, timedelta

import polars as pl

from sams.core.codes import canonical_code, canonical_market
from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.services._fold import fold, fold_to_price_basis, last, window_high
from sams.storage.repos.ledger import Account, Position, SimAccountRepo, SimOrderRepo
from sams.storage.repos.theses import Thesis, ThesisRepo

#: 日线一次取多少天：盖住 MA60（60 个交易日）与一段正常持有期。
#: 与 `valuation` 的窗口无关，所以它自己算一次：那边只带它合成结论要的那几路出门。
DAILY_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 400

#: 持仓不足这么多根日 K 时，「持仓期最高价」没有意义（第一根就是最高价，回撤恒为 0）。
TRAILING_MIN_BARS = 3

#: MA60 要哪一列：`indicators=("ma",)` 产出 ma5/ma10/ma20/ma60/ma120/ma250
_MA_COLUMN = "ma60"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Target:
    """一趟求值里的一个对象。`user_id` 也在里面：展开出来的一切都必须说清是谁的。"""

    user_id: str
    market: str
    code: str
    account_id: str | None = None
    thesis_id: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Snapshot:
    """一个对象在一次求值里的全部事实，**现价口径 + 标的币种**。

    每个数与它的 `*_on` 同进同出：一条「跌破止损」必须说得出这个价是哪天的。
    所有 `None` 都是「没有这个数」而不是 0——0 在阈值语言里是一个能触发止损的值。
    """

    market: str
    code: str
    #: 显示名（`symbols` 里那一列；读不到就用代码本身，屏幕上不能是空的）
    name: str = ""
    currency: str = ""
    report_currency: str = ""
    account_id: str | None = None
    account_name: str = ""
    thesis_id: str | None = None
    #: 现价（过闸日线的裸收盘）与它属于哪天
    price: float | None = None
    price_on: date | None = None
    price_age_days: int | None = None
    #: 当日开盘与前收盘：`urgent`（跳空低开破位）与 `price.cross_*` 靠这两个数。
    #: 库里只有日线，所以开盘价来自 `BarRepo`，不是快照流（§3 的裁剪）
    day_open: float | None = None
    prev_close: float | None = None
    #: 持仓（None ⇒ 这个目标此刻没有持仓，持仓类模板一律不判）
    qty: float | None = None
    avg_cost: float | None = None
    opened_on: date | None = None
    holding_days: int | None = None
    #: 持仓期最高价，折回现价口径。不足 `TRAILING_MIN_BARS` 根 ⇒ None
    holding_high: float | None = None
    #: 折回现价口径的技术位
    weekly_ma20: float | None = None
    daily_ma60: float | None = None
    atr: float | None = None
    #: 论上那几列 + 本次合成的结论
    stop_line: float | None = None
    target_price: float | None = None
    odds: float | None = None
    score: float | None = None
    items: tuple[tuple[str, float], ...] = ()
    #: 最近一期财报的披露日（`scheduled.reevaluate` 的输入）
    disclosed_on: date | None = None
    #: 保证金账户：`notes` 据此说「爆仓线可能先到」
    margin_enabled: bool = False
    #: 这一路缺了什么（进告警正文、`--explain` 与 `--json`）
    missing: tuple[str, ...] = ()


class RuleData:
    """把八个仓库/服务包成两个动作。构造参数全是 `Container` 上已有的字段。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        valuation,
        analysis,
        bars,
        symbols,
        accounts: SimAccountRepo,
        ledger: SimOrderRepo,
        theses: ThesisRepo,
        watchlists,
        settings,
    ) -> None:
        self.valuation = valuation
        self.analysis = analysis
        self.bars = bars
        self.symbols = symbols
        self.accounts = accounts
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.theses = theses
        self.watchlists = watchlists
        self.settings = settings

    # --- 展开 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def expand(self, rule) -> list[Target]:
        """一条规则 → 一组对象。`enabled` 与冷却由调用方管，这里只管形状。"""
        kind = rule.target_type
        if kind == "symbol":
            market, _, code = rule.target_ref.partition("/")
            normalized = canonical_market(market)
            return [
                Target(
                    rule.user_id,
                    normalized,
                    canonical_code(normalized, code),
                    rule.account_id,
                    rule.thesis_id,
                )
            ]
        if kind == "watchlist":
            return [
                Target(rule.user_id, market, code, rule.account_id, rule.thesis_id)
                for market, code in self.watchlists.codes(rule.user_id)
            ]
        if kind == "position":
            if rule.account_id is None:
                raise ValidationError(
                    f"规则 {rule.name} 是持仓类却没有账户：去重键含 account_id，"
                    "缺了它同一只票在两个账户里的破位会互相吞（§10.1）"
                )
            return [
                Target(rule.user_id, row.market, row.code, rule.account_id, rule.thesis_id)
                for row in self.holdings(rule.account_id)
            ]
        if kind == "thesis":
            thesis = self._thesis_of_rule(rule)
            if thesis is None:
                return []
            return [Target(rule.user_id, thesis.market, thesis.code, rule.account_id, thesis.id)]
        raise ValidationError(
            f"target_type={kind} 这一层没有展开方式：组合级规则要的是每日组合快照（U28），"
            "而库里还没有那张表"
        )

    def holdings(self, account_id: str) -> list[Position]:
        """当前持仓（不含已清仓的 0 股行）。一趟里同一个账户只读一次由引擎保证：
        它先调这里拿表，再把每一行交给 `read(..., position=...)`。"""
        return [row for row in self.ledger.positions(account_id) if row.qty > 0]

    def thesis_for(self, target: Target) -> Thesis | None:
        if target.thesis_id is None:
            return None
        return self.theses.get(target.user_id, target.thesis_id)

    def _thesis_of_rule(self, rule) -> Thesis | None:
        return self.theses.get(rule.user_id, rule.thesis_id or rule.target_ref)

    # --- 读取 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def read(
        self,
        target: Target,
        *,
        as_of: date,
        position: Position | None = None,
    ) -> Snapshot:
        """装配一个对象此刻的全部数。三路读取各有各的理由（见模块开头）。

        `position` 是引擎从 `holdings()` 里带来的那一行：带着它就不必读第二次，
        而两次读之间规则可以被改，那样算出来的成本与屏幕上那一条不是同一个数。
        """
        thesis = self.thesis_for(target)
        valuation = self.valuation.read(
            target.market,
            target.code,
            as_of=as_of,
            style=thesis.style if thesis is not None else "value",
        )
        daily = self.analysis.series(
            target.market,
            target.code,
            start=as_of - timedelta(days=DAILY_LOOKBACK_DAYS),
            end=as_of,
            indicators=("ma",),
        )
        account = self._account(target)
        missing: list[str] = []
        if valuation.price is None:
            missing.append("没有过闸的现价：这一趟不判任何价格类条件")
        return Snapshot(
            market=target.market,
            code=target.code,
            name=self._name(target),
            currency=self._currency(target, position),
            report_currency=(
                account.report_currency
                if account is not None
                else self.settings.report_currency
            ),
            account_id=target.account_id,
            account_name=account.name if account is not None else "",
            thesis_id=target.thesis_id,
            price=valuation.price,
            price_on=valuation.price_on,
            price_age_days=valuation.price_age_days,
            day_open=self._day_open(target, as_of),
            prev_close=self._prev_close(daily.table, valuation.price),
            qty=None if position is None else position.qty,
            avg_cost=None if position is None else position.avg_cost,
            opened_on=None if position is None else _date_of(position.open_ts),
            holding_days=_holding_days(position, as_of),
            holding_high=_holding_high(daily.table, valuation.price, position, as_of),
            weekly_ma20=valuation.weekly_ma20,
            daily_ma60=_fold_line(daily.table, _MA_COLUMN, valuation.price),
            atr=valuation.atr,
            stop_line=_stop_line(thesis, valuation),
            target_price=valuation.plan.target.low if valuation.plan.target.ok else None,
            odds=valuation.plan.odds.value,
            score=valuation.composite.score,
            items=tuple(
                sorted(
                    (item.key, item.value)
                    for item in valuation.composite.items
                    if item.value is not None
                )
            ),
            disclosed_on=valuation.fundamental.published_on,
            margin_enabled=bool(account is not None and account.margin_enabled),
            missing=tuple(missing),
        )

    def _name(self, target: Target) -> str:
        got = self.symbols.get(target.market, target.code) or {}
        return str(got.get("name") or target.code)

    def _account(self, target: Target) -> Account | None:
        if target.account_id is None:
            return None
        return self.accounts.get(target.user_id, target.account_id)

    def _currency(self, target: Target, position: Position | None) -> str:
        """标的币种：有持仓就用账本那一列，没有才退回市场配置。

        顺序反过来的话，一个把 `symbols.currency` 写歪的旧行会让阈值带上错误的币种后缀
        （§5.4 规则 3 禁止未标注币种的金额，而**标错**比不标更难查）。
        """
        if position is not None:
            return position.currency
        cfg = self.settings.markets.get(target.market)
        return cfg.currency if cfg is not None else ""

    def _day_open(self, target: Target, as_of: date) -> float | None:
        """当日开盘：库里只有不复权日线，裸价**就是**现价口径（不折）。"""
        frame = self.bars.load(target.market, [target.code], start=as_of, end=as_of)
        if frame.height == 0:
            return None
        value = frame["open"][0]
        return None if value is None else float(value)

    def _prev_close(self, table: pl.DataFrame, price: float | None) -> float | None:
        """前收盘折回现价口径：跨除权日时裸价那个「昨天」不是今天可比的那个「昨天」。"""
        if table.height < 2 or "close_hfq" not in table.columns:
            return None
        before = float(table["close_hfq"][-2])
        return fold_to_price_basis(before, fold(price, last(table, "close_hfq")))


# --- 纯算式（没有 IO，所以模块级）-------------------------------------------------------------


def _fold_line(table: pl.DataFrame, column: str, price: float | None) -> float | None:
    """把后复权口径的一条指标折到现价口径：因子来自**这条**日线序列的当日两个收盘。"""
    return fold_to_price_basis(last(table, column), fold(price, last(table, "close_hfq")))


def _holding_high(
    table: pl.DataFrame, price: float | None, position: Position | None, as_of: date
) -> float | None:
    """持仓期最高价（现价口径）。没有持仓、没有建仓时刻、或不足三根 ⇒ None（见常量注释）。"""
    if position is None or position.open_ts is None or table.height == 0:
        return None
    window = table.filter(pl.col("date") >= position.open_ts.date())
    if window.height < TRAILING_MIN_BARS:
        return None
    factor = fold(price, last(table, "close_hfq"))
    return fold_to_price_basis(window_high(window, window.height), factor)


def _stop_line(thesis: Thesis | None, valuation) -> float | None:
    """止损线优先用**论上那一列**（用户确认过的数），没有才退回本次合成的那一条。

    顺序反过来的话，用户手工把线抬到成本（`stop.breakeven` 之后常见）会被下一次自动
    合成覆盖——屏幕上那条线就不是他填的那一条了。
    """
    if thesis is not None and thesis.stop_price is not None:
        return thesis.stop_price
    return valuation.plan.stop.low if valuation.plan.stop.ok else None


def _date_of(moment) -> date | None:
    return None if moment is None else moment.date()


def _holding_days(position: Position | None, as_of: date) -> int | None:
    if position is None or position.open_ts is None:
        return None
    return max(0, (as_of - position.open_ts.date()).days)
```

### T-7：`templates.py` 的第一批——绝对价、成本百分比、移动止损

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/templates.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_templates_stops.py`

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_templates_stops.py`

```python
"""止损模板：纯函数，手搓 `Snapshot`。

这一文件里的每一枚都是 §10.2 那条强制验收的一部分——**除权日不许误报**。
所以断言里出现的都是「折回之后的数」，没有一处自己再乘一遍因子（那正是外套穿两件的样子）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest

from sams.core.rules import HIT, PRE, URGENT, Verdict
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot
from sams.services.rules.templates import (
    Ask,
    Outcome,
    money,
    stop_fixed,
    stop_pct,
    stop_stage,
    stop_trailing,
)


def snap(**over) -> Snapshot:
    base = {
        "market": "A",
        "code": "601088",
        "name": "中国神华",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "report_currency": "CNY",
        "price": 10.0,
        "price_on": date(2026, 10, 20),
        "prev_close": 10.2,
        "avg_cost": 12.0,
        "qty": 100.0,
    }
    base.update(over)
    return Snapshot(**base)


def ask(params: dict, **over) -> Ask:
    return Ask(snapshot=snap(**over), params=params)


def test_stage_order_looks_at_the_gap_first() -> None:
    """开盘在前收盘之上时不许报 `urgent`：那一档的意义正是「一开盘就没机会」。"""
    assert stop_stage(snap(day_open=10.8), 10.5) == HIT
    assert stop_stage(snap(day_open=9.8, prev_close=10.6), 10.5) == URGENT
    assert stop_stage(snap(day_open=10.7, prev_close=10.6), 10.62) == PRE
    assert stop_stage(snap(price=12.0), 10.0) is None
    assert stop_stage(snap(price=None), 10.0) is None


def test_money_always_carries_a_currency() -> None:
    """§5.4 规则 3：金额类阈值不允许不带币种。缺币种时明写「元」也不许留空。"""
    assert money(10.5, "USD") == "10.50 USD"
    assert money(None, "USD") == "未知"
    assert money(10.0, "") == "10.00 元"


def test_a_fixed_stop_names_the_line_and_the_currency() -> None:
    got = stop_fixed(ask({"price": 10.5}))
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert got.hit.threshold_text == "10.50 CNY"
    assert got.hit.value_text == "10.00 CNY"


def test_a_fixed_stop_just_above_the_price_is_a_pre_warning() -> None:
    """再跌 2% 就到线 ⇒ `pre`：这一档不是「破了」，屏幕上必须说得不一样。"""
    got = stop_fixed(ask({"price": 10.1}))
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == PRE


def test_a_fixed_stop_well_above_the_price_says_nothing() -> None:
    got = stop_fixed(ask({"price": 9.0}))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert got.why_not == ""  # 没触发是正常状态，不是「缺输入」，不该有一句话


def test_an_absent_price_is_named_instead_of_silently_false() -> None:
    """没有现价 ⇒ `why_not` 有话：屏幕上「没触发」与「这一趟没法判」必须是两句话。"""
    got = stop_fixed(ask({"price": 10.5}, price=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "现价" in got.why_not


def test_a_gap_down_below_the_line_is_urgent_and_says_so() -> None:
    got = stop_fixed(ask({"price": 10.5}, day_open=9.8, prev_close=10.6))
    assert got.hit.stage == URGENT
    assert "开盘" in got.hit.why and "前收盘" in got.hit.why


def test_a_pct_stop_is_measured_from_cost_not_from_the_market() -> None:
    got = stop_pct(ask({"pct": 0.08}))  # 成本 12 → 线 11.04，现价 10 已破
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert got.hit.threshold == pytest.approx(12.0 * (1 - 0.08))
    assert "成本" in got.hit.why


def test_a_pct_stop_without_a_position_explains_itself() -> None:
    got = stop_pct(ask({"pct": 0.08}, avg_cost=None, qty=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "成本" in got.why_not


def test_trailing_uses_the_holding_period_high() -> None:
    got = stop_trailing(ask({"pct": 0.10}, holding_high=15.0))
    assert got.hit.threshold == pytest.approx(13.5)
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT  # 现价 10 < 13.5


def test_trailing_refuses_to_invent_a_high_when_the_position_is_new() -> None:
    """`holding_high=None`（不足三根 K，T-6 给的 None）时不许拿现价凑。

    拿现价凑的话回撤恒为 0，那条线永远贴在现价上方 10%，第一次呼吸就报止损。
    """
    got = stop_trailing(ask({"pct": 0.10}, holding_high=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "最高价" in got.why_not


def test_a_verdict_is_rejected_when_the_stage_is_not_one_of_three() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Exception, match="stage"):
        Verdict(stage="breaking", value=1.0, threshold=2.0, value_text="1", threshold_text="2")
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_templates_stops.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.templates'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/templates.py`（第一部分）

```python
"""每个 kind 一个纯函数：给一份 `Snapshot` 与那几条参数，判一次（spec §10.3）。

`Outcome` 而不是 `Verdict | None`：**「没触发」与「判不了」是两句话**。前者不该有任何
输出，后者必须给出那句话——否则一条因为缺数据而从没响过的规则，在屏幕上与一条
工作正常的规则长得一模一样（§1 那张表的最后一行）。

所有阈值都在**现价口径 + 标的币种**里比（§5.4 规则 3）：折回因子是 T-6 乘好的，
这里再乘一次就是两件外套，除权日会只有一侧折回。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass

from sams.core.rules import HIT, PRE, URGENT, Verdict
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot

#: 「快到了」那一档有多近：再跌 2% 到线就报 `pre`（§10.2 举例的那个刻度）。
#: 写成一处而不是每个模板的参数：九种止损各配一个 `pre_band`，用户要回答一个他自己
#: 也说不清的数，而配出九个值就是没有值。
PRE_BAND = 0.02


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Ask:
    """一次求值的输入：谁的哪个对象、此刻的数、这条规则的参数。"""

    snapshot: Snapshot
    params: Mapping[str, object]
    #: 这条规则挂着的那条论（若有）。模板读它的价位与状态时**只**从这里读，不自己去查库：
    #: `Snapshot` 已经查过一次，两次之间规则还能被改
    thesis: object | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Outcome:
    """一个模板的一次输出。`hit` 为 None 时 `why_not` 是那句话，或者那句空。"""

    hit: Verdict | None = None
    why_not: str = ""


def money(value: float | None, currency: str) -> str:
    """阈值与现价的措辞：数后面永远跟着币种（§5.4 规则 3）。

    金额类阈值不允许不带币种，而 A 股是元、美股是美元——屏幕上写「止损 100」的那条规则，
    用户在手机上会按另一种货币去理解它。
    """
    if value is None:
        return "未知"
    return f"{value:.2f} {currency or '元'}"


def stop_stage(
    snapshot: Snapshot, line: float, *, pre_band: float = PRE_BAND
) -> str | None:
    """三档判定。顺序不能调：先看跳空，再看是否已破，最后才看「快到了」。

    `urgent` 要求**前收盘在线上方**而**开盘在线下方**——只有这种形状才是「一开盘就没机会」。
    只看开盘的话，一条早就破了的线每趟都会升格成 urgent，那一档的意义被用光。
    """
    price = snapshot.price
    if price is None:
        return None
    if (
        snapshot.day_open is not None
        and snapshot.prev_close is not None
        and snapshot.day_open < line
        and snapshot.prev_close >= line
    ):
        return URGENT
    if price < line:
        return HIT
    if price < line * (1.0 + pre_band):
        return PRE
    return None


def _no_price(snapshot: Snapshot) -> str:
    return (
        f"没有 {snapshot.market}/{snapshot.code} 过闸的现价：这一趟不判任何价格类条件"
        "（§10.1 的新鲜度过滤器拦掉的就是这种读数）"
    )


def _gap_note(snapshot: Snapshot, line: float, stage: str) -> str:
    """`urgent` 那句话要把「开盘 vs 前收盘」两个数一起说出来：只报现价的话，
    读的人看不出这是跳空而不是慢慢跌过去的。"""
    if stage != URGENT:
        return ""
    return (
        f"；开盘 {money(snapshot.day_open, snapshot.currency)} 直接在线 "
        f"{money(line, snapshot.currency)} 下方（前收盘 "
        f"{money(snapshot.prev_close, snapshot.currency)}），这一跳不是盘中跌过去的"
    )


def _hit(snapshot: Snapshot, line: float, stage: str, why: str) -> Outcome:
    return Outcome(
        Verdict(
            stage=stage,
            value=snapshot.price,
            threshold=line,
            value_text=money(snapshot.price, snapshot.currency),
            threshold_text=money(line, snapshot.currency),
            why=why + _gap_note(snapshot, line, stage),
        )
    )


def stop_fixed(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.fixed`：跌破绝对价即报。线就是用户填的那个数（现价口径、标的币种）。"""
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    line = float(ask.params["price"])
    stage = stop_stage(snapshot, line)
    if stage is None:
        return Outcome()
    return _hit(
        snapshot, line, stage, f"绝对价止损线 {money(line, snapshot.currency)}"
    )


def stop_pct(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.pct`：成本下浮 x%。没有持仓就没有成本，这一条不判而不是判成 0 成本。"""
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.avg_cost is None or snapshot.avg_cost <= 0:
        return Outcome(
            why_not=f"这一笔在账户 {snapshot.account_id} 里没有成本：`stop.pct` 的线"
            "由成本算出，缺了它就没有线"
        )
    pct = float(ask.params["pct"])
    line = snapshot.avg_cost * (1.0 - pct)
    stage = stop_stage(snapshot, line)
    if stage is None:
        return Outcome()
    return _hit(
        snapshot,
        line,
        stage,
        f"成本 {money(snapshot.avg_cost, snapshot.currency)} 下浮 {pct:.1%}"
        f" ⇒ 线 {money(line, snapshot.currency)}",
    )


def stop_trailing(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.trailing`：持仓期最高价回撤 x%。最高价来自 T-6，已是现价口径。"""
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.holding_high is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="没有可用的持仓期最高价（持仓不足三根日 K 时它没有意义）："
            "拿现价凑会让回撤恒为 0，那条线贴在现价上方，第一次呼吸就报止损"
        )
    pct = float(ask.params["pct"])
    line = snapshot.holding_high * (1.0 - pct)
    stage = stop_stage(snapshot, line)
    if stage is None:
        return Outcome()
    return _hit(
        snapshot,
        line,
        stage,
        f"持仓期最高价 {money(snapshot.holding_high, snapshot.currency)}"
        f" 回撤 {pct:.1%} ⇒ 线 {money(line, snapshot.currency)}",
    )


TABLE: dict[str, Callable[[Ask], Outcome]] = {
    "stop.fixed": stop_fixed,
    "stop.pct": stop_pct,
    "stop.trailing": stop_trailing,
}
```

`TABLE` 在 T-8 ~ T-11 里逐段补全；T-11 最后一枚用例断言它的键集合等于 `core.rules.RULE_KINDS`——少一个 kind 就是那条规则永远不会被求值。

### T-8：ATR、均线、保本——那三条也要折回

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/sams/services/rules/templates.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_templates_stops.py`（追加）

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试**（追加）

```python
from sams.services.rules.templates import stop_atr, stop_breakeven, stop_ma


def test_atr_stop_is_cost_minus_multiple_of_the_folded_atr() -> None:
    """ATR 是后复权口径算出来的，必须已经被 T-6 折回。这里再乘一次就是两件外套。"""
    got = stop_atr(ask({"multiple": 2.0}, atr=0.5))  # 12 − 1.0 = 11，现价 10 ⇒ hit
    assert got.hit.threshold == pytest.approx(11.0)
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert "ATR" in got.hit.why


def test_a_missing_atr_is_named_instead_of_defaulting_to_zero() -> None:
    """缺 ATR 时不许按 0 算：那条线会正好贴在成本上，现价低于成本一分都报止损。"""
    got = stop_atr(ask({"multiple": 2.0}, atr=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "ATR" in got.why_not


def test_an_atr_multiple_that_pushes_the_line_below_zero_is_refused() -> None:
    """线算到 0 以下是一条永不触发的规则，而屏幕上它显示着「启用」。"""
    got = stop_atr(ask({"multiple": 10.0}, atr=1.5))  # 12 − 15 < 0
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "永远不会触发" in got.why_not


def test_ma_stop_takes_the_weekly_line_by_default() -> None:
    got = stop_ma(ask({"basis": "weekly_ma20"}, weekly_ma20=11.0))
    assert got.hit.threshold == pytest.approx(11.0)
    assert "周 MA20" in got.hit.why


def test_ma_stop_can_take_the_daily_sixty() -> None:
    got = stop_ma(ask({"basis": "daily_ma60"}, daily_ma60=9.0))
    assert got.hit is None  # 现价 10 > 9，也还没进 9×1.02 之内 ⇒ 两档都不报


def test_a_missing_ma_names_which_line_is_missing() -> None:
    got = stop_ma(ask({"basis": "daily_ma60"}, daily_ma60=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "日 MA60" in got.why_not


def test_breakeven_raises_the_line_after_the_gain_was_once_reached() -> None:
    """浮盈「曾经」到过 x% 才把线抬到成本——判据是持仓期最高价，不是今天的现价。

    用今天的现价判的话，一条涨过 20% 又跌回去的仓反而「没触发」，而它正是这一条要救的那种。
    """
    got = stop_breakeven(ask({"trigger_pct": 0.15}, holding_high=15.0))
    assert got.hit.threshold == pytest.approx(12.0)  # 抬到成本
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert "成本" in got.hit.why


def test_breakeven_stays_quiet_when_the_trigger_was_never_hit() -> None:
    got = stop_breakeven(ask({"trigger_pct": 0.15}, holding_high=12.5))  # 只涨过 4.2%
    assert got.hit is None
    assert got.why_not == ""


def test_breakeven_without_a_position_has_no_cost_to_breakeven_against() -> None:
    got = stop_breakeven(
        ask({"trigger_pct": 0.15}, avg_cost=None, qty=None, holding_high=None)
    )
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "成本" in got.why_not
```

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现**（追加到 `templates.py`，并把三行加进 `TABLE`）

```python
def stop_atr(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.atr`：成本 − 倍数×ATR14。ATR 由 T-6 折回现价口径，这里只做算术。"""
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.avg_cost is None or snapshot.avg_cost <= 0:
        return Outcome(why_not="没有成本就没有这条线：`stop.atr` 是从成本往下量倍数")
    if snapshot.atr is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="这一路没有 ATR14（日 K 不足 14 根，或那几根没过数据闸）："
            "把它当 0 会让线正好贴在成本上，现价低一分都报止损"
        )
    multiple = float(ask.params["multiple"])
    line = snapshot.avg_cost - multiple * snapshot.atr
    if line <= 0:
        return Outcome(
            why_not=f"倍数 {multiple:g} 把线算到了 {money(line, snapshot.currency)}："
            "一条在 0 以下的线永远不会触发，这条规则等于没写"
        )
    stage = stop_stage(snapshot, line)
    if stage is None:
        return Outcome()
    return _hit(
        snapshot,
        line,
        stage,
        f"成本 {money(snapshot.avg_cost, snapshot.currency)} − {multiple:g}×ATR14"
        f"（{money(snapshot.atr, snapshot.currency)}，已从后复权折回现价口径）"
        f" ⇒ 线 {money(line, snapshot.currency)}",
    )


#: `basis` 那两个取值 → (取 `Snapshot` 的哪个字段, 那句话里的名字)
#: 名字写在这里而不是拼出来：「日 MA60」是给人读的词，字段名是给代码读的，
#: 把 `daily_ma60` 直接上屏等于让用户去猜那是什么意思。
_MA_LINES = {"weekly_ma20": ("weekly_ma20", "周 MA20"), "daily_ma60": ("daily_ma60", "日 MA60")}


def stop_ma(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.ma`：跌破均线。两条线都在 T-6 折回过，用哪一条由参数说。"""
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    field, label = _MA_LINES[str(ask.params["basis"])]
    line = getattr(snapshot, field)
    if line is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not=f"没有 {label}：这一路的窗口不够长，或那几根日 K 没过数据闸"
        )
    stage = stop_stage(snapshot, line)
    if stage is None:
        return Outcome()
    return _hit(
        snapshot, line, stage, f"现价跌破 {label} {money(line, snapshot.currency)}"
    )


def stop_breakeven(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.breakeven`：浮盈曾经到过 x% 之后，把线抬到成本。

    触发判据是**持仓期最高价**而不是今天的现价（上面那枚测试的理由）：这一条的意义就是
    「已经赚过，别把利润全还回去」，而赚过的那一刻是唯一可查的事实。
    线抬到成本 ⇒ 现价只要低于成本就 `hit`，正是字面意思。
    """
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.avg_cost is None or snapshot.avg_cost <= 0:
        return Outcome(why_not="保本止损要把线抬到成本，而这一笔没有成本可读")
    if snapshot.holding_high is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="没有持仓期最高价：判不出「曾经到过 x%」，宁可不报也不猜"
        )
    trigger = float(ask.params["trigger_pct"])
    reached = snapshot.holding_high / snapshot.avg_cost - 1.0
    if reached < trigger:
        return Outcome()
    line = snapshot.avg_cost
    stage = stop_stage(snapshot, line)
    if stage is None:
        return Outcome()
    return _hit(
        snapshot,
        line,
        stage,
        f"持仓期最高 {money(snapshot.holding_high, snapshot.currency)}"
        f"（成本上方 {reached:.1%}，已过 {trigger:.1%}）⇒ 线已抬到成本 "
        f"{money(line, snapshot.currency)}",
    )
```

```python
TABLE: dict[str, Callable[[Ask], Outcome]] = {
    "stop.fixed": stop_fixed,
    "stop.pct": stop_pct,
    "stop.trailing": stop_trailing,
    "stop.atr": stop_atr,
    "stop.ma": stop_ma,
    "stop.breakeven": stop_breakeven,
}
```

```python
from sams.services.rules.templates import stop_time


def test_time_stop_fires_after_n_days_without_the_expected_gain() -> None:
    got = stop_time(ask({"days": 30, "max_gain_pct": 0.0}, holding_days=45))
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert got.hit.threshold_text == "0.0%"
    assert "45" in got.hit.why


def test_time_stop_stays_quiet_while_the_position_is_younger_than_the_window() -> None:
    got = stop_time(ask({"days": 30, "max_gain_pct": 0.0}, holding_days=12))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert got.why_not == ""  # 没到期不是「判不了」，是这件事还没到


def test_time_stop_does_not_fire_when_the_gain_was_reached() -> None:
    got = stop_time(
        ask({"days": 30, "max_gain_pct": 0.0}, holding_days=45, price=12.5)
    )  # 成本 12 ⇒ +4.2%，已达预期
    assert got.hit is None


def test_time_stop_names_a_missing_open_date_instead_of_counting_zero_days() -> None:
    """`holding_days=None` 时不许当 0：那条规则会永远不响，而屏幕上写着「启用」。"""
    got = stop_time(ask({"days": 30, "max_gain_pct": 0.0}, holding_days=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "建仓" in got.why_not


def test_time_stop_asks_for_a_review_and_not_for_a_sell() -> None:
    """§9.3：系统只提示，不替用户决定平仓。这一条的文案就是它的实现。"""
    got = stop_time(ask({"days": 30, "max_gain_pct": 0.0}, holding_days=45))
    assert "复核" in got.hit.why
    assert "不替你平仓" in got.hit.why
```

```python
"""读论的那几条：`stop.thesis_drift`、`tp.tiered`、`price.cross_*`、`thesis.*`、`scheduled.*`。

与止损那文件的区别只有一件事——这里每一枚都可能因为「论没读到」而判不了，
而判不了必须在 `why_not` 里有话（§1 那张表：屏幕上「没触发」与「从没比过」长得一样）。

`thesis` 用的是仓库那个真 `Thesis`，不是手搓的替身：字段名写错时这里就该红。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from sams.core.rules import HIT, PRE
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot
from sams.services.rules.templates import Ask, stop_thesis_drift
from sams.storage.repos.theses import INVALIDATED, TRACKING, Thesis


def snap(**over) -> Snapshot:
    base = {
        "market": "A",
        "code": "601088",
        "name": "中国神华",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "price": 10.0,
        "price_on": date(2026, 10, 20),
        "prev_close": 10.2,
        "avg_cost": 12.0,
        "qty": 100.0,
    }
    base.update(over)
    return Snapshot(**base)


def thesis(**over) -> Thesis:
    base = {
        "id": "th-1",
        "user_id": "u-1",
        "market": "A",
        "code": "601088",
        "style": "value",
        "status": TRACKING,
        "stop_price": 9.0,
        "target_price": 15.0,
        "reward_risk": 2.5,
        "baseline_score": 60.0,
    }
    base.update(over)
    return Thesis(**base)


def ask(params: dict, snapshot_over: dict, the=None) -> Ask:
    return Ask(
        snapshot=snap(**snapshot_over), params=params, thesis=the or thesis()
    )


DRIFT = {}  # 这一条无参数：`validate_params` 补完就是 {}


def test_a_dead_thesis_is_drift_even_when_the_price_never_moved() -> None:
    """§10.2 那一行「价格没跌但理由已失效」：现价 10 高于止损线，论却已被判死。"""
    got = stop_thesis_drift(ask(DRIFT, {}, the=thesis(status=INVALIDATED)))
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert INVALIDATED in got.hit.why


def test_a_score_drop_across_a_tier_is_named_with_both_numbers() -> None:
    got = stop_thesis_drift(ask(DRIFT, {"score": 35.0}))  # 60 → 35：3 档 → 1 档
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert "60" in got.hit.why and "35" in got.hit.why
    assert got.hit.threshold == 60.0


def test_a_score_wobble_inside_one_tier_is_not_drift() -> None:
    """78 → 61 一档都没掉（都是 3 档）：分项分自带窗口噪声，跨档才是能被说清的变化。

    这一枚必须同时给一个**没崩的赔率**（2.6 ≥ 建仓时的 2.5）：不给的话它落到「判不了」
    那一支，测试会因为错误的原因通过。
    """
    got = stop_thesis_drift(
        ask(DRIFT, {"score": 61.0, "odds": 2.6}, the=thesis(baseline_score=78.0))
    )
    assert got.hit is None
    assert got.why_not == ""


def test_collapsed_odds_is_a_reason_by_itself() -> None:
    got = stop_thesis_drift(ask(DRIFT, {"odds": 1.1}))  # 建仓时 2.5 倍
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert "赔率" in got.hit.why
    assert got.hit.threshold == 2.5


def test_all_three_findings_are_printed_in_one_sentence() -> None:
    got = stop_thesis_drift(
        ask(DRIFT, {"score": 20.0, "odds": 0.8}, the=thesis(status=INVALIDATED))
    )
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.why.count("；") == 2
    assert "复核" in got.hit.why  # §9.3：只提示，不替用户平仓


def test_a_live_thesis_with_a_fresh_score_reports_nothing() -> None:
    got = stop_thesis_drift(ask(DRIFT, {"score": 62.0, "odds": 2.6}))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert got.why_not == ""


def test_an_unread_thesis_is_refused_rather_than_judged_alive() -> None:
    """规则挂着 `needs_thesis` 却没读到论 ⇒ 这是取数出了问题，不是「理由还在」。"""
    got = stop_thesis_drift(ask(DRIFT, {}, the=None))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "论" in got.why_not


def test_a_baseline_without_a_new_score_is_a_gap_not_a_pass() -> None:
    """有基线分却合成出新分数失败 ⇒ 不许默默放过，那正是「从没比过」。"""
    got = stop_thesis_drift(
        ask(DRIFT, {"score": None, "odds": None}, the=thesis(reward_risk=None))
    )
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "判不了" in got.why_not
```

- [ ] **Step 4：写实现**（追加到 `templates.py`，两行加进 `TABLE`）

```python
def stop_time(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.time`：持仓 N 天没到预期涨幅 ⇒ 提醒复核。

    天数是**自然日**（`holding_days` = as_of − 建仓日）而不是交易日：判据来自持仓的
    `open_ts`，换成交易日要引 `CalendarService`，而 T-6 刻意没让取数依赖日历。
    保守方向也在这里：自然日永远不少于交易日，所以这一条只会**早**响不会晚响。

    它是 §10.2 说的「阴跌不触发价格线时的兜底」——价格类模板全沉默时唯一会说话的一条。
    """
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.avg_cost is None or snapshot.avg_cost <= 0:
        return Outcome(why_not="时间止损要比「现在的涨幅」，而这一笔没有成本可读")
    if snapshot.holding_days is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="没有建仓时间：`holding_days` 来自持仓的 open_ts，"
            "缺了它「拿了多久」无从判起（当 0 会让这条规则永远不响）"
        )
    days = int(ask.params["days"])
    if snapshot.holding_days < days:
        return Outcome()
    ceiling = float(ask.params["max_gain_pct"])
    gain = snapshot.price / snapshot.avg_cost - 1.0
    if gain >= ceiling:
        return Outcome()
    opened = f"（{snapshot.opened_on} 建仓）" if snapshot.opened_on else ""
    return Outcome(
        Verdict(
            stage=HIT,
            value=gain,
            threshold=ceiling,
            value_text=f"{gain:.1%}",
            threshold_text=f"{ceiling:.1%}",
            why=f"持有 {snapshot.holding_days} 天{opened}（自然日口径），涨幅 {gain:.1%} "
            f"没到 {ceiling:.1%}：价格线一条都没破，这一条是兜底 ⇒ 请复核理由还在不在"
            "（系统只提示，不替你平仓，§9.3）",
        )
    )


#: 「理由已经没了」的两个状态。名字从 `storage` 引，不在这里抄一遍字符串：
#: 词表在 0001 与 `theses.py` 里各有一份的时候，漂移就是这么来的。
_DEAD_STATUSES = (INVALIDATED, HIT_STOP)


def stop_thesis_drift(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`stop.thesis_drift`：打分掉档 / 赔率崩塌 / 论已被判死——三条里任意一条成立即报。

    这一条与 `thesis.drift`（T-11，周复检掉档）读同一份基线，但**级别不同、时机不同**：
    那一头的意义是「每周有一次人来看一眼」，所以只有掉档；这一头是止损位的替代判断，
    所以论一旦被判死（状态）也立刻算数——价格还没跌，而记账的理由已经没了。

    三个数都读不到时不是「没触发」而是 `why_not` 有话：`Outcome` 的两句话在这里最值钱。
    """
    thesis = ask.thesis
    if thesis is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="这条规则要求挂一条论（`needs_thesis`），而这一趟没读到它："
            "没有「理由」在手，就不存在「理由失效」——这是取数出了问题，不是判过没事"
        )
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    findings: list[str] = []
    unknowns: list[str] = []
    value: float | None = None
    threshold: float | None = None
    value_text = "—"
    threshold_text = "—"

    if thesis.status in _DEAD_STATUSES:
        findings.append(
            f"这条论已被记为 {thesis.status}：价格没跌，但账上写着的理由已经没了"
        )

    base_tier = tier_of(thesis.baseline_score)
    new_tier = tier_of(snapshot.score)
    if base_tier is not None:
        if new_tier is None:
            unknowns.append("有建仓时的基线分，这一趟却没合成出新分数（掉档判不了）")
        elif new_tier < base_tier:
            findings.append(
                f"打分从 {thesis.baseline_score:g} 掉到 {snapshot.score:g}"
                f"（{base_tier} 档 → {new_tier} 档）"
            )
            value, threshold = snapshot.score, thesis.baseline_score
            value_text = f"{snapshot.score:g} 分"
            threshold_text = f"{thesis.baseline_score:g} 分"

    if thesis.reward_risk and snapshot.odds is not None:
        if snapshot.odds < thesis.reward_risk:
            findings.append(
                f"赔率从建仓时的 {thesis.reward_risk:g} 倍掉到 {snapshot.odds:g} 倍："
                "同一个止损位下，这笔仓的期望已经支撑不住"
            )
            if value is None:
                value, threshold = snapshot.odds, thesis.reward_risk
                value_text = f"{snapshot.odds:g} 倍"
                threshold_text = f"{thesis.reward_risk:g} 倍"
    elif thesis.reward_risk and not unknowns:
        unknowns.append("建仓时记着赔率，这一趟没有合成出来（崩塌与否判不了）")

    if findings:
        return Outcome(
            Verdict(
                stage=HIT,
                value=value,
                threshold=threshold,
                value_text=value_text,
                threshold_text=threshold_text,
                why="；".join(findings) + " ⇒ 请复核这条论（系统只提示，不替你平仓，§9.3）",
            )
        )
    if unknowns:
        return Outcome(why_not="；".join(unknowns))
    return Outcome()
```

```python
TABLE: dict[str, Callable[[Ask], Outcome]] = {
    ...,
    "stop.time": stop_time,
    "stop.thesis_drift": stop_thesis_drift,
}
```

文件顶部那组 import 里再加一行 `import pytest`（下面两枚用了 `pytest.approx`）：

```python
import pytest
```

```python
from sams.services.rules.templates import price_cross_above, price_cross_below, tp_tiered


def test_a_halfway_gain_is_a_pre_warning_with_the_tier_named() -> None:
    """成本 12 → 目标 15 这一段走了 60%：报「到 50% 那一档」，级别是 `pre`。"""
    got = tp_tiered(
        ask({"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"price": 13.8, "target_price": 15.0})
    )
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == PRE
    assert "50%" in got.hit.why
    assert got.hit.value == pytest.approx((13.8 - 12.0) / (15.0 - 12.0))


def test_the_target_price_itself_is_a_hit_not_a_pre() -> None:
    """最后一档必须是 `hit`：同一去重键上只有更响的一档能穿冷却（D94），
    两档都记 `hit` 的话，到了目标价那一条会被半小时前的 50% 吞掉。"""
    got = tp_tiered(ask({"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"price": 15.2, "target_price": 15.0}))
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert "目标价" in got.hit.why


def test_a_gain_below_the_first_tier_says_nothing() -> None:
    got = tp_tiered(ask({"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"price": 12.6, "target_price": 15.0}))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert got.why_not == ""


def test_a_target_at_or_below_cost_is_refused_as_a_scale_that_cannot_be_divided() -> None:
    got = tp_tiered(ask({"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"price": 10.0, "target_price": 12.0}))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "成本" in got.why_not


def test_no_target_price_is_named_instead_of_defaulting_to_the_price() -> None:
    """拿现价当目标价 ⇒ 进度恒为 1 ⇒ 每趟都报「到了目标价」。"""
    got = tp_tiered(ask({"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"target_price": None}))
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "目标价" in got.why_not


def test_cross_below_needs_the_previous_close_to_call_it_a_cross() -> None:
    got = price_cross_below(ask({"price": 10.5}), {"prev_close": None})
    assert got.hit is None
    assert "前收盘" in got.why_not


def test_a_price_already_below_the_line_at_the_previous_close_is_not_a_cross() -> None:
    """这就是那一枚防刷屏的用例：昨天就在下面，今天不算「穿」。

    只用现价判的话，这条规则每个交易日收盘后都响一次，而 §10.1 的去重键里没有
    「第几天」这一维——刷屏会在三天内把用户对该通道的信任磨光。
    """
    got = price_cross_below(ask({"price": 10.5}), {"prev_close": 10.4, "price": 10.1})
    assert got.hit is None


def test_a_downward_cross_is_reported_with_both_sides_and_the_currency() -> None:
    got = price_cross_below(ask({"price": 10.5}), {"prev_close": 10.6, "price": 10.1})
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert got.hit.threshold_text == "10.50 CNY"
    assert "前收盘" in got.hit.why and "现价" in got.hit.why


def test_cross_above_is_the_mirror_image() -> None:
    got = price_cross_above(ask({"price": 10.5}), {"prev_close": 10.4, "price": 10.8})
    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert got.hit.threshold == 10.5


def test_cross_above_stays_quiet_when_we_were_already_above() -> None:
    got = price_cross_above(ask({"price": 10.5}), {"prev_close": 10.9, "price": 11.0})
    assert got.hit is None
```

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现**（追加到 `templates.py`）

```python
def tp_tiered(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`tp.tiered`：把「成本 → 目标价」这一段切成几档，走到哪一档报哪一档。

    刻度是**那一段涨幅**而不是目标价本身：0.5 的意思是「目标涨幅走了一半」，
    与 §10.2 那句「解决涨一半不敢拿」对上。只报**已达到的最高一档**（不是每一档各报一次），
    因为一趟求值发两条同键告警会被冷却吃掉第二条，屏幕上看到的是「只报了一条」——
    与其让人猜，不如一次只说一格，下一格由下一趟来说。

    档位用 `pre`/`hit` 两个阶段而不是一律 `hit`：见上面那枚测试，这是 D94 唯一的
    非止损用法。中间档之间仍共用一个去重键，30 分钟内第二档会被压——那是 §10.1
    的节流语义（键里没有档位），而两档之间通常隔着几天，不是半小时。
    """
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.avg_cost is None or snapshot.avg_cost <= 0:
        return Outcome(why_not="分批止盈的刻度从成本起算，而这一笔没有成本可读")
    if snapshot.target_price is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="没有目标价：分档量的是「成本 → 目标价」那一段，缺终点就没有刻度"
            "（拿现价当终点会让进度恒为 1，每趟都报到了）"
        )
    span = snapshot.target_price - snapshot.avg_cost
    if span <= 0:
        return Outcome(
            why_not=f"目标价 {money(snapshot.target_price, snapshot.currency)} 不高于成本 "
            f"{money(snapshot.avg_cost, snapshot.currency)}：那一段是 0 或负的，"
            "百分比刻度在这里没有意义（要盯跌到某个价请用 stop.fixed）"
        )
    tiers = ask.params["tiers"]  # `validate_params` 已保证递增且每档 > 0
    progress = (snapshot.price - snapshot.avg_cost) / span
    reached = [tier for tier in tiers if progress >= tier]
    if not reached:
        return Outcome()
    top = reached[-1]
    final = progress >= tiers[-1]
    remaining = len(tiers) - len(reached)
    tail = "已到最后一档 ⇒ 目标价" if final else f"还剩 {remaining} 档"
    return Outcome(
        Verdict(
            stage=HIT if final else PRE,
            value=progress,
            threshold=top,
            value_text=f"{progress:.1%}",
            threshold_text=f"{top:.0%}",
            why=f"成本 {money(snapshot.avg_cost, snapshot.currency)} 到目标价 "
            f"{money(snapshot.target_price, snapshot.currency)} 这一段走完 {progress:.1%}"
            f"（已达 {top:.0%} 那一档，{tail}）",
        )
    )


def _cross(ask: Ask, *, upward: bool) -> Outcome:
    """两个 `price.cross_*` 的共用体：穿过去 = 前一根还在另一侧，这一根到了这一侧。

    `>=` / `<=` 里带等号：前收盘**正好**压在线上也算在另一侧起跳——那是最典型的
    回踩确认，把它算成「已经穿过了」会让这一天不报。
    """
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return Outcome(why_not=_no_price(snapshot))
    if snapshot.prev_close is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="没有前收盘：「穿」说的是从今天上一根到今天的这一跳，"
            "只用现价判的话，一条早就在下面的线每个交易日都会重报一次"
        )
    line = float(ask.params["price"])
    if upward:
        crossed = snapshot.prev_close <= line < snapshot.price
    else:
        crossed = snapshot.prev_close >= line > snapshot.price
    if not crossed:
        return Outcome()
    verb = "上穿" if upward else "下穿"
    return Outcome(
        Verdict(
            stage=HIT,
            value=snapshot.price,
            threshold=line,
            value_text=money(snapshot.price, snapshot.currency),
            threshold_text=money(line, snapshot.currency),
            why=f"前收盘 {money(snapshot.prev_close, snapshot.currency)} 在线 "
            f"{money(line, snapshot.currency)} 的另侧，现价 "
            f"{money(snapshot.price, snapshot.currency)} 已{verb}",
        )
    )


def price_cross_below(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`price.cross_below`：跌穿指定价（§9.2 派生的「进入买入区间」用它）。"""
    return _cross(ask, upward=False)


def price_cross_above(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`price.cross_above`：涨穿指定价（§9.2 派生的「达到目标价」用它）。"""
    return _cross(ask, upward=True)
```

```python
TABLE: dict[str, Callable[[Ask], Outcome]] = {
    ...,
    "tp.tiered": tp_tiered,
    "price.cross_below": price_cross_below,
    "price.cross_above": price_cross_above,
}
```

```python
from sams.analysis.valuation.scoring import item_label
from sams.core.rules import RULE_KINDS
from sams.services.rules.templates import (
    TABLE,
    scheduled_reevaluate,
    thesis_drift,

def test_item_label_names_the_known_items_and_falls_back_to_the_key() -> None:
    """屏幕上要写「市赚率 PR」而不是 `pr`：键是身份，标签是给人读的词。

    这个函数是 T-11 唯一碰 `analysis` 的地方，而它存在的原因就是「不许在 `templates.py`
    里再抄一份 `_ITEM_LABELS`」——两份名单必然有一天不一样。

def test_odds_below_reports_with_the_multiplier_unit() -> None:

    assert got.hit is not None
    assert got.hit.stage == HIT
    assert got.hit.threshold_text == "1.5 倍"
    assert got.hit.value_text == "1.1 倍"


def test_odds_at_the_threshold_is_not_below_it() -> None:

    assert got.hit is None

def test_a_missing_odds_is_never_read_as_zero() -> None:

    got = thesis_odds_below(ask({"threshold": 1.5}, {"odds": None}))

def test_a_dropped_item_is_named_by_label_with_both_scores() -> None:

        ask(DRIFT, {"items": (("pr", 40.0), ("roe", 80.0))},

    got = thesis_drift(
        ask(DRIFT, {"items": (("pr", 40.0),), "score": 40.0},
            the=thesis(baseline_items=(), baseline_score=None))
    )

def test_an_item_that_was_not_composed_this_time_is_a_gap_not_a_pass() -> None:

    )
```

```python
def item_label(key: str) -> str:
    """分项键 → 给人读的名字。认不出的键原样返回：那说明加了一项却没登记名字，
    屏幕上宁可看到 `main_inflow` 也不要看到空白或者一个猜出来的中文词。"""
    return _ITEM_LABELS.get(key, key)
```

（`_ITEM_LABELS` 保持私有一张表，公开的只有这一个读它的函数——名单还是一份。）

- [ ] **Step 4：写实现**（追加到 `templates.py`，把三行加进 `TABLE`）

```python
def thesis_odds_below(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`thesis.odds_below`：合成出来的赔率跌破阈值（§9.2 派生默认 1.5 倍）。

    阈值来自参数而不是 `thesis.reward_risk`：后者是**建仓时**的赔率（那是历史事实），
    这一条要判的是「现在还算不算得过来」，两者相减的部分归 `stop.thesis_drift`。
    """
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.odds is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="这一趟没合成出赔率（止损位或目标价缺一个就算不出）："
            "把它当 0 会让所有赔率规则在数据缺的那天全体触发"
        )
    threshold = float(ask.params["threshold"])
    if snapshot.odds >= threshold:
        return Outcome()
    return Outcome(
        Verdict(
            stage=HIT,
            value=snapshot.odds,
            threshold=threshold,
            value_text=f"{snapshot.odds:g} 倍",
            threshold_text=f"{threshold:g} 倍",
            why=f"现价口径下赔率 {snapshot.odds:g} 倍，低于阈值 {threshold:g} 倍："
            "往上到目标价的空间已经换不起往下到止损位的空间了",
        )
    )


def thesis_drift(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`thesis.drift`：§9.3 周复检那一半的判据——**分项**掉过一档就算漂移。

    读的是 `baseline_items`（T-4 那三列之一）而不是总分：spec 的原话是「分项打分下滑
    超过一档」。总分只用来进 `Verdict.value`（`--json` 那边要一个数），因为总分掉一档
    可能是一个大权重项掉了 3 分，而分项那句话说的是「哪一项变了」。

    与 `stop.thesis_drift` 的分工写在那一头的 docstring 里：同一份基线，两个时机两个级别。
    """
    thesis = ask.thesis
    if thesis is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="这一条要比的是这条论的基线，而这一趟没读到论本身："
            "没有基线在手就没有「漂移」可判"
        )
    baseline = dict(thesis.baseline_items)
    if not baseline and thesis.baseline_score is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="这条论还没有基线（周复检第一次跑时建立，T-16）："
            "从没比过不等于没漂移，在这里报「观点未漂移」是一句没人验证过的谎（D96）"
        )
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    now = dict(snapshot.items)
    dropped: list[str] = []
    unseen: list[str] = []
    for key, base in sorted(baseline.items()):
        label = item_label(key)
        if key not in now:
            unseen.append(label)
            continue
        was, new = tier_of(base), tier_of(now[key])
        if was is None or new is None:
            unseen.append(label)
            continue
        if new < was:
            dropped.append(
                f"{label} {base:g} → {now[key]:g}（{was} 档 → {new} 档）"
            )
    if dropped:
        return Outcome(
            Verdict(
                stage=HIT,
                value=snapshot.score,
                threshold=thesis.baseline_score,
                value_text=(
                    f"{snapshot.score:g} 分" if snapshot.score is not None else "—"
                ),
                threshold_text=(
                    f"{thesis.baseline_score:g} 分"
                    if thesis.baseline_score is not None
                    else "—"
                ),
                why="；".join(dropped)
                + " ⇒ 观点漂移，请复核（系统只提示，不替你平仓，§9.3）",
            )
        )
    if unseen:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="这些分项这一趟没合成出来，比不了：" + "、".join(unseen)
        )
    return Outcome()


def scheduled_reevaluate(ask: Ask) -> Outcome:
    """`scheduled.reevaluate`：最近 `within_days` 天内出了新财报 ⇒ 提醒复核这条论。

    「这一趟是哪一天」用 `Snapshot.price_on`：引擎按 `as_of` 取数，而那一天最后一个过闸
    读数的日子就是它——再引一个日期字段会让两个「天」有机会不一致。

    窗口内每个交易日都会各判中一次，这不是漏：§10.1 的 `cooldown_min` 管的是刷屏，
    不是「提醒到用户看过为止」。T-15 派生这一条时默认带 `no_open_alert`（T-12 的过滤器：
    同一只票已有未处理告警就不重复推），于是这一段窗口实际只响第一次，之后由用户的
    「已处理」或新告警来清。
    """
    snapshot = ask.snapshot
    if snapshot.disclosed_on is None:
        return Outcome(
            why_not="没有财报披露日（这一路基本面没读到 published_on）："
            "事件驱动的那一端没有事件"
        )
    if snapshot.price_on is None:
        return Outcome(why_not="这一趟没有基准日：比较披露日需要「今天是哪天」")
    within = int(ask.params["within_days"])
    age = (snapshot.price_on - snapshot.disclosed_on).days
    if age < 0:
        return Outcome(
            why_not=f"披露日 {snapshot.disclosed_on} 晚于这一趟的基准日 "
            f"{snapshot.price_on}：宁可不报，也不报一条来自未来的提醒"
        )
    if age > within:
        return Outcome()
    return Outcome(
        Verdict(
            stage=HIT,
            value=float(age),
            threshold=float(within),
            value_text=f"{age} 天前",
            threshold_text=f"{within} 天窗口",
            why=f"{snapshot.disclosed_on} 披露了新财报（{age} 天前，在 {within} 天窗口内）"
            " ⇒ 该用新数据复核这条论：ROE、增速、PR 的输入都换了",
        )
    )
```

```python
TABLE: dict[str, Callable[[Ask], Outcome]] = {
    ...,
    "thesis.odds_below": thesis_odds_below,
    "thesis.drift": thesis_drift,
    "scheduled.reevaluate": scheduled_reevaluate,
}
```

`templates.py` 的 import 到这里应当是：

```python
from sams.analysis.valuation.scoring import item_label
from sams.core.rules import HIT, PRE, URGENT, RULE_KINDS, Verdict, tier_of
from sams.storage.repos.theses import HIT_STOP, INVALIDATED, Thesis
```

（最后一行只是类型注解用得到 `Thesis` 时才加；`Ask.thesis` 的注解从 `object | None`
收紧成 `Thesis | None`，并把 `dataclass` 那一段的注释改掉——注解错了比没有注解更误导人。
收紧之后 T-9/T-11 里 `thesis.status`、`thesis.baseline_items` 这些读法才有静态检查兜着。）

## 阶段 E：过滤、执行约束、一趟引擎与维护面（T-12 ~ T-17）

### T-12：`filters.py`——四个开关，外加仓库那一句「有没有未处理的」

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/filters.py`
- Modify: `src/sams/storage/repos/alerts.py`（加 `latest_open`）
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_filters.py`
- Test: `tests/storage/test_alerts_repo.py`（追加）

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_filters.py`

```python
"""四个过滤开关（§10.1「降噪主力」）。全部是纯函数：上下文由引擎备好传进来。

这里贯穿一条取向：**判不出来就放行**。这些开关存在的理由是少推噪音，不是拦截信号；
「日历读不到 ⇒ 一律压制」会让所有规则在那一天全体沉默，而那正是 §1 表里最贵的一行——
屏幕上「一切正常」，实际是一条真止损没人听见。所以每一枚测试都同时钉两侧：
该压的压住，该放的（上下文缺失）一定放。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta

import pytest

from sams.core.rules import HIT, PRE, URGENT, Verdict, template_for
from sams.core.timeutil import Session
from sams.services.rules.filters import Gate, OpenAlert, run_filters
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot

MORNING_OPEN = datetime(2026, 10, 20, 9, 30, tzinfo=UTC)
SESSIONS = (Session("morning", MORNING_OPEN, MORNING_OPEN + timedelta(hours=2)),)


def at(minutes: int) -> Gate:
    """开盘前后 `minutes` 分钟那个时刻的 Gate：负数是开盘**前**（集合竞价）。"""
    return Gate(now=MORNING_OPEN + timedelta(minutes=minutes), sessions=SESSIONS)


def snap(**over) -> Snapshot:
    base = {
        "market": "A",
        "code": "601088",
        "name": "中国神华",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "price": 9.8,
        "price_on": date(2026, 10, 20),
        "price_age_days": 0,
        "prev_close": 10.2,
    }
    base.update(over)
    return Snapshot(**base)


def hit(stage: str = HIT) -> Verdict:
    return Verdict(
        stage=stage,
        value=9.8,
        threshold=10.0,
        value_text="9.80 CNY",
        threshold_text="10.00 CNY",
    )


def run(
    filters: dict,
    gate: Gate,
    snapshot: Snapshot | None = None,
    verdict: Verdict | None = None,
    kind: str = "stop.fixed",
) -> str:
    return run_filters(
        filters,
        snapshot=snapshot or snap(),
        gate=gate,
        verdict=verdict or hit(),
        built=template_for(kind),
    )


def test_all_four_switches_off_suppresses_nothing() -> None:
    """默认全关（`validate_filters({})` 给的就是全关）：过滤器是 opt-in 的降噪，不是默认行为。"""
    assert run({}, Gate()) == ""


def test_time_window_suppresses_inside_the_minutes_after_the_open() -> None:
    got = run({"time_window": True}, at(3))
    assert "开盘" in got and "5" in got


def test_time_window_covers_the_call_auction_minutes_too() -> None:
    """§10.1 的原话是「开盘前 5 分钟」：A 股 9:15–9:25 的竞价价不是成交出来的价。"""
    assert run({"time_window": True}, at(-3)) != ""


def test_time_window_passes_once_the_window_is_over() -> None:
    assert run({"time_window": True}, at(6)) == ""


def test_time_window_leaves_a_non_price_kind_alone() -> None:
    """披露日提醒不是价格类：开盘那 5 分钟压不掉一条「该复核这条论」。"""
    assert run({"time_window": True}, at(1), kind="scheduled.reevaluate") == ""


def test_time_window_passes_when_the_sessions_are_not_read() -> None:
    assert run({"time_window": True}, Gate(now=MORNING_OPEN)) == ""


def test_data_freshness_suppresses_a_price_older_than_the_bound() -> None:
    got = run({"data_freshness": True}, Gate(), snap(price_age_days=4))
    assert "4 天前" in got and "2026-10-20" in got


def test_data_freshness_passes_at_the_bound_it_defends() -> None:
    """3 天正好是那个界：`>` 而不是 `>=`，否则周末一次的读数天天被压。"""
    assert run({"data_freshness": True}, Gate(), snap(price_age_days=3)) == ""


def test_data_freshness_suppresses_a_missing_price() -> None:
    assert "现价" in run({"data_freshness": True}, Gate(), snap(price=None, price_age_days=None))


def test_data_freshness_leaves_a_thesis_kind_alone() -> None:
    """赔率那条规则读的是价位的比，不是「今天的价格」——行情旧三天不影响它说话。"""
    stale = snap(price_age_days=30)
    assert run({"data_freshness": True}, Gate(), stale, kind="thesis.odds_below") == ""


def test_exclude_halt_suppresses_when_the_last_bar_is_before_the_last_session() -> None:
    """判据是「该市场最近一个交易日没有这根行」，不是停牌的权威数据。

    §10.3 把停复牌列为 M5 的事件类：现在没有那个源。这里用的是同方向的证据，
    而它命中新股未上市与节假日未同步时**也应该**压制——三种解释都指向「今天没有价」。
    """
    gate = Gate(last_session_on=date(2026, 10, 20))
    got = run({"exclude_halt": True}, gate, snap(price_on=date(2026, 10, 16)))
    assert "2026-10-20" in got and "2026-10-16" in got


def test_exclude_halt_passes_when_the_bar_is_current() -> None:
    assert run({"exclude_halt": True}, Gate(last_session_on=date(2026, 10, 20))) == ""


def test_no_open_alert_suppresses_a_repeat_of_the_same_stage() -> None:
    gate = Gate(open_alert=OpenAlert(id="al-1", stage=HIT, on=date(2026, 10, 20)))
    got = run({"no_open_alert": True}, gate)
    assert "al-1" in got and "未处理" in got


def test_no_open_alert_suppresses_a_quieter_repeat() -> None:
    gate = Gate(open_alert=OpenAlert(id="al-1", stage=URGENT, on=date(2026, 10, 20)))
    assert run({"no_open_alert": True}, gate, verdict=hit(HIT)) != ""


def test_no_open_alert_lets_an_escalation_through() -> None:
    """与 D94 同一个道理：未处理的那条是 `pre`，现在真跌破了 ⇒ 压不掉。

    「已有一条没点掉的预警」恰恰是不能用来堵住止损的理由。
    """
    gate = Gate(open_alert=OpenAlert(id="al-1", stage=PRE, on=date(2026, 10, 20)))
    assert run({"no_open_alert": True}, gate, verdict=hit(HIT)) == ""


def test_no_open_alert_passes_when_nothing_is_open() -> None:
    assert run({"no_open_alert": True}, Gate()) == ""


def test_the_first_matching_switch_wins_and_says_which() -> None:
    """两个开关同时命中时只回一句：`suppressed` 的投递记录只有一行，理由要能读。"""
    gate = Gate(
        open_alert=OpenAlert(id="al-1", stage=HIT, on=date(2026, 10, 20)),
        last_session_on=date(2026, 10, 27),
    )
    got = run({"exclude_halt": True, "no_open_alert": True}, gate)
    assert got.startswith("该市场")  # `exclude_halt` 在 `FILTER_KEYS` 里排在前面


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "switch", ["time_window", "data_freshness", "exclude_halt", "no_open_alert"]
)
def test_every_declared_switch_has_an_implementation(switch: str) -> None:
    """名单来自 `core.rules.FILTER_KEYS`：这里少一个实现，那一头就能配出一个恒真的开关。"""
    assert run({switch: True}, Gate()) == ""  # 空 Gate ⇒ 全部放行，函数不抛


def test_the_gate_is_a_value_object_not_a_repo() -> None:
    """过滤器不许自己查库——否则这四个开关在没库的测试里跑不起来，而 §10.1 说评估是纯函数。"""
    import inspect

    from sams.services.rules import filters

    assert "storage" not in inspect.getsource(filters)
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_filters.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.filters'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/filters.py`

```python
"""四个过滤开关（spec §10.1）。纯函数，判不出来就放行。

引擎（T-14）的次序是：模板判中 ⇒ 过一遍这里 ⇒ 冷却 ⇒ 投递。过滤器在冷却之前，
因为「这条本来就不该推」与「这条被半小时内的上一条压了」是两件事，
投递记录里那一句必须说得出是哪一种。

`Gate` 是引擎备好的「这一趟之外的世界」：现在的时刻、那个市场的时段、
该市场最近一个交易日、那只票上未处理的那条告警。这四样都不在 `Snapshot` 里——
`Snapshot` 是**那个对象**的事实，`Gate` 是**这一次求值**的环境。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta

from sams.core.rules import (
    FILTER_KEYS,
    FILTER_MINUTES,
    STALE_PRICE_DAYS,
    Template,
    Verdict,
    escalates,
)
from sams.core.timeutil import Session
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OpenAlert:
    """那只票上一条**未处理**的告警（`no_open_alert` 的输入）。

    只带三个字段：过滤器要的是「有没有、那一档多响」，而正文与通道属于 `sams alert show`。
    """

    id: str
    stage: str
    on: date


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Gate:
    now: datetime | None = None
    sessions: tuple[Session, ...] = ()
    #: 该市场最近一个已开市的日子（`CalendarService` 给）。None ⇒ 日历没读到
    last_session_on: date | None = None
    open_alert: OpenAlert | None = None


def time_window(snapshot: Snapshot, gate: Gate, verdict: Verdict, built: Template) -> str:
    """开盘前后各 5 分钟内不推价格类：集合竞价那个价不是成交出来的价（§10.1）。

    向两侧各伸 5 分钟而不是只卡开盘之后：A 股 9:15–9:25 是竞价，未匹配的大额买卖会在
    那十分钟里造出一个假破位。窗口外的时段（午后、收盘后）一概放行。
    """
    if not built.price_class or gate.now is None or not gate.sessions:
        return ""
    window = timedelta(minutes=FILTER_MINUTES)
    for session in gate.sessions:
        if session.open - window <= gate.now < session.open + window:
            return (
                f"{session.name} 开盘前后 {FILTER_MINUTES} 分钟内不推价格类："
                f"开盘 {session.open:%H:%M}，现在 {gate.now:%H:%M}，"
                "那几分钟里的价不是连续竞价的价"
            )
    return ""


def data_freshness(snapshot: Snapshot, gate: Gate, verdict: Verdict, built: Template) -> str:
    """行情太旧 ⇒ 不推。界是 `STALE_PRICE_DAYS`，判据是 `>`（3 天正好过）。"""
    if not built.price_class:
        return ""
    if snapshot.price is None:
        return f"{snapshot.market}/{snapshot.code} 没有过闸现价：价格类条件这一趟不推"
    age = snapshot.price_age_days
    if age is None:
        return ""  # 不知道多旧 ⇒ 放行（取向见模块开头）
    if age > STALE_PRICE_DAYS:
        return (
            f"现价是 {age} 天前的读数（{snapshot.price_on}），超过 {STALE_PRICE_DAYS} 天："
            "拿它报「跌破止损」是在报一个已经过去的价格"
        )
    return ""


def exclude_halt(snapshot: Snapshot, gate: Gate, verdict: Verdict, built: Template) -> str:
    """当天没有行 ⇒ 不推。见上面那枚测试：这是证据而不是权威数据。"""
    if not built.price_class or gate.last_session_on is None:
        return ""
    if snapshot.price_on is not None and snapshot.price_on < gate.last_session_on:
        return (
            f"该市场最近一个交易日是 {gate.last_session_on}，而 "
            f"{snapshot.market}/{snapshot.code} 最后一根日线在 {snapshot.price_on}："
            "那天没有行（停牌、未上市或没同步到），价格类推送在这里没有意义"
        )
    return ""


def no_open_alert(snapshot: Snapshot, gate: Gate, verdict: Verdict, built: Template) -> str:
    """同票已有一条未处理告警时不重复推——除非这一条更响（与 D94 同一个理由）。"""
    open_alert = gate.open_alert
    if open_alert is None:
        return ""
    if escalates(open_alert.stage, verdict.stage):
        return ""
    return (
        f"已有一条未处理的 {open_alert.stage} 告警（{open_alert.id}，"
        f"{open_alert.on}）在这一只票上；这一条 {verdict.stage} 不比它更响 ⇒ "
        "先处理那一条（`sams alert handle`）"
    )


FILTERS: dict[str, Callable[[Snapshot, Gate, Verdict, Template], str]] = {
    "time_window": time_window,
    "data_freshness": data_freshness,
    "exclude_halt": exclude_halt,
    "no_open_alert": no_open_alert,
}


def run_filters(
    filters: Mapping[str, object],
    *,
    snapshot: Snapshot,
    gate: Gate,
    verdict: Verdict,
    built: Template,
) -> str:
    """按 `FILTER_KEYS` 的顺序过一遍**被打开**的开关，第一个命中的那句返回；全过 ⇒ 空串。

    顺序走 `FILTER_KEYS` 而不是 `filters` 的键序：库里那串 JSON 的键序是历史（用户改过
    两次开关，最后一次写进去的顺序谁都没在意），而压制理由要能复算。
    """
    for key in FILTER_KEYS:
        if not bool(filters.get(key)):
            continue
        reason = FILTERS[key](snapshot, gate, verdict, built)
        if reason:
            return reason
    return ""
```

- [ ] **Step 4：写失败的测试**（追加到 `tests/storage/test_alerts_repo.py`，用现成的 `repo` / `seeded_user` / `CREATE_KW` fixture）

```python
def test_latest_open_finds_the_newest_unhandled_alert_on_that_symbol(
    repo, seeded_user
) -> None:
    from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    first = repo.create(user_id=seeded_user, **{**CREATE_KW, "stage": "pre"})
    repo.set_status(first.id, "handled")
    second = repo.create(user_id=seeded_user, **{**CREATE_KW, "stage": "hit"})
    # 另一只票的一条：不该被算进来
    repo.create(user_id=seeded_user, **{**CREATE_KW, "code": "600519", "stage": "urgent"})
    found = repo.latest_open(seeded_user, CREATE_KW["market"], CREATE_KW["code"])
    assert found is not None and found.id == second.id and found.stage == "hit"


def test_latest_open_returns_none_once_the_last_one_is_dismissed(repo, seeded_user) -> None:
    got = repo.create(user_id=seeded_user, **CREATE_KW)
    assert repo.latest_open(seeded_user, CREATE_KW["market"], CREATE_KW["code"]) is not None
    repo.set_status(got.id, "dismissed", note="误报")
    assert repo.latest_open(seeded_user, CREATE_KW["market"], CREATE_KW["code"]) is None


def test_latest_open_ignores_another_users_alerts(repo, seeded_user) -> None:
    repo.create(user_id="u-somebody-else", **CREATE_KW)
    assert repo.latest_open(seeded_user, CREATE_KW["market"], CREATE_KW["code"]) is None
```

- [ ] **Step 5：给 `AlertRepo` 加那一句读**（`src/sams/storage/repos/alerts.py`，放在 T-3 的 `latest_for` 之后）

```python
    def latest_open(self, user_id: str, market: str, code: str) -> Alert | None:
        """该 (用户, 市场, 代码) 上最新一条**未处理**告警——`no_open_alert` 的输入（§10.1）。

        刻意不带 `account_id`：这一条过滤器的语义是「这个用户在这只票上已经有一条没点掉的
        提醒」，把账户算进去的话，A 账户里那条未处理的止损会挡不住 B 账户的第二条推送，
        用户收到两条内容一样、只有账户名不同的告警。
        与 `latest_for` 的分工也在这里：那一头管冷却（同键同账户），这一头管刷屏（同票同人）。
        """
        rows = self.db.query(
            f"SELECT {_ALERT_COLUMNS} FROM alerts"
            " WHERE user_id = ? AND market = ? AND code = ? AND status = 'new'"
            " ORDER BY triggered_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
            [user_id, market, code],
        )
        return self._to_alert(rows.to_dicts()[0]) if rows.height else None
```

### T-13：`notes.py`——把「能不能执行」写进告警正文

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/notes.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_notes.py`

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_notes.py`

```python
"""§10.2 的执行约束文案。约束绑**标的市场**，不绑账户（D21）。

这一文件里的每一句话都是为了让那条告警**不误导决策**：「止损已破」后面如果不跟一句
「而今天买的可能今天就卖不掉」，用户会按一个执行不了的计划去操作。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

import pytest

from sams.core.rules import HIT, Verdict
from sams.services.rules.notes import Fx, constraint_lines, holding_pnl
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot


def snap(**over) -> Snapshot:
    base = {
        "market": "A",
        "code": "601088",
        "name": "中国神华",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "report_currency": "CNY",
        "price": 9.8,
        "price_on": date(2026, 10, 20),
        "prev_close": 10.2,
        "avg_cost": 12.0,
        "qty": 100.0,
        "opened_on": date(2025, 3, 3),
    }
    base.update(over)
    return Snapshot(**base)


def verdict() -> Verdict:
    return Verdict(
        stage=HIT,
        value=9.8,
        threshold=10.0,
        value_text="9.80 CNY",
        threshold_text="10.00 CNY",
    )


def test_a_position_opened_today_is_marked_t_plus_one() -> None:
    got = constraint_lines(snap(opened_on=date(2026, 10, 20)))
    assert any("T+1" in line and "今日买入" in line for line in got)


def test_an_older_position_is_not_marked_t_plus_one() -> None:
    assert not any("T+1" in line for line in constraint_lines(snap()))


def test_a_us_symbol_is_t_plus_zero_and_says_so_only_when_it_matters() -> None:
    """美股没有 T+1 可说：写一条「可当日卖出」是废话，而不写才是对的。"""
    assert not any("T+1" in line for line in constraint_lines(snap(market="US", code="AAPL")))


def test_a_limit_down_print_is_named_with_its_own_band() -> None:
    """主板 -10%、创业板/科创板 -20%：那句话里的数必须来自 `daily_price_limit`，不许写死。"""
    got = constraint_lines(snap(prev_close=10.9, price=9.81))
    assert any("跌停" in line and "10%" in line for line in got)


def test_a_wide_band_symbol_uses_the_wider_band() -> None:
    got = constraint_lines(snap(code="300750", prev_close=100.0, price=80.5))
    assert any("20%" in line for line in got)


def test_a_normal_down_day_is_not_called_a_limit_down() -> None:
    assert not any("跌停" in line for line in constraint_lines(snap()))


def test_a_margin_account_gets_the_maintenance_note() -> None:
    got = constraint_lines(snap(margin_enabled=True))
    assert any("维持担保比例" in line for line in got)
    assert any("M5" in line for line in got)  # 说清这一条只是提醒，规则本身没实现


def test_a_non_margin_account_does_not_claim_it() -> None:
    assert not any("维持担保" in line for line in constraint_lines(snap()))


def test_the_pnl_line_always_carries_the_symbol_currency() -> None:
    """§5.4 规则 3：金额一律带币种。-220.00 后面什么都不跟的话，美股那条会被读成人民币。"""
    got = holding_pnl(snap(currency="USD", report_currency="USD"))
    assert got.endswith("USD")
    assert got.startswith("浮亏 ")


def test_the_pnl_line_adds_the_report_currency_when_a_rate_is_given() -> None:
    got = holding_pnl(
        snap(currency="USD", report_currency="CNY"),
        fx=Fx(currency="CNY", rate=7.08, on=date(2026, 10, 17)),
    )
    assert "USD" in got and "折 CNY" in got and "7.08" in got and "2026-10-17" in got


def test_a_cross_currency_pnl_without_a_rate_says_which_pair_is_missing() -> None:
    """不许默默省掉折合值：那一屏上只有标的币种，用户会以为那就是汇总口径。"""
    got = holding_pnl(snap(currency="USD", report_currency="CNY"))
    assert "USD→CNY" in got and "没读到" in got


def test_a_report_currency_that_matches_needs_no_conversion() -> None:
    got = holding_pnl(snap(), fx=None)
    assert got.endswith("CNY") and "折" not in got


def test_a_missing_position_produces_no_lines() -> None:
    assert constraint_lines(snap(qty=None, avg_cost=None)) == ()
    assert holding_pnl(snap(qty=None)) == ""
    assert holding_pnl(snap(avg_cost=None)) == ""


def test_a_missing_prev_close_is_not_called_a_limit_down() -> None:
    """没有前收盘就判不出涨跌幅：这时宁可不写那一行（取向与 T-12 一致）。"""
    assert not any("跌停" in line for line in constraint_lines(snap(prev_close=None)))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("band", [0.0985, 0.0999])
def test_the_limit_down_test_has_room_for_rounding(band: float) -> None:
    """涨跌停价是**四舍五入到分**的：10.9 × 0.9 = 9.81 而交易所可能挂 9.81/9.82。

    判据留 1.5% 的余量（`LIMIT_DOWN_TOLERANCE`），差一分就判不出「封板」的那一种必须算进去——
    报「跌停封板」误了执行，比漏报一次更伤决策吗？不，漏报才是问题：误报让用户以为卖不掉，
    而封板判据的余量方向是**放宽**（更容易判成封板），所以这里选 1.5% 并写进注释。
    """
    got = constraint_lines(snap(prev_close=10.0, price=10.0 * (1 - band)))
    assert any("跌停" in line for line in got)
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_notes.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.notes'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/notes.py`

```python
"""执行约束文案（spec §10.2「必须写进告警文案」那一段）。纯函数，只产字符串。

为什么这一层单独存在：约束绑的是**标的所属市场**，不是账户（D21）——同一个账户里的
A 股仍 T+1、美股仍 T+0。把这几句话拼在投递那里，就会有两个通道各自拼一遍，
而 §10.2 要的正是「每条告警都带、措辞一致」。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date

from sams.core.codes import canonical_market, daily_price_limit
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot

#: 每个市场的交割规则。这一张表在这里是唯一的一份：`CalendarService` 管时段，
#: `daily_price_limit` 管涨跌幅，都不管交割，所以 T+1 这个词只能出现在这个文件里。
#: 加一种市场就加一行——没有配置项是故意的：交割规则是市场事实，不是用户偏好。
_SETTLEMENT = {"A": "T+1", "HK": "T+0", "US": "T+0"}

#: 跌停判据的余量：交易所的涨跌停价是四舍五入到分的，正好卡在板上的那一分可能差 0.1%。
#: 方向选**放宽**（更容易判成封板）：这一句的作用是「别按这个价去挂单」，
#: 漏一句的代价是用户按一个执行不了的价去操作，多一句的代价只是文案长一点。
_LIMIT_DOWN_TOLERANCE = 0.015


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Fx:
    """汇总币种那一边：一个汇率与它的日期。由引擎从 `FxRepo.latest` 读来（T-14）。"""

    currency: str
    rate: float
    on: date
    #: `FxRepo` 那个数过了多少天：过期汇率要说得出自己过期（§5.4 规则 3 的另一半）
    age_days: int = 0


def _limit_down(snapshot: Snapshot) -> tuple[bool, float | None]:
    """现价是否贴在跌停板上。返回 (是否, 那一板的幅度)——幅度给文案用，不写死 10%。"""
    band = daily_price_limit(snapshot.market, snapshot.code)
    if band is None or snapshot.prev_close is None or snapshot.price is None:
        return False, band
    floor = snapshot.prev_close * (1.0 - band)
    return snapshot.price <= floor * (1.0 + _LIMIT_DOWN_TOLERANCE), band


def constraint_lines(snapshot: Snapshot) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """这一笔今天能不能按告警里的价执行，一次说完。没有一条约束时返回空元组。

    顺序固定（交割 → 涨跌幅 → 保证金）：读的人要先知道「今天卖不卖得掉」，
    再知道「卖得掉的话有没有额外的线在前面」。
    """
    lines: list[str] = []
    market = canonical_market(snapshot.market)
    rule = _SETTLEMENT.get(market)
    if (
        rule == "T+1"
        and snapshot.opened_on is not None
        and snapshot.price_on is not None
        and snapshot.opened_on == snapshot.price_on
    ):
        lines.append(
            "今日买入，受 T+1 限制无法当日卖出：这一条要等下一个交易日执行"
        )
    down, band = _limit_down(snapshot)
    if down and band is not None:
        lines.append(
            f"跌停封板（{band:.0%}）附近，可能无法按告警里的价成交："
            "挂单排队与撤单时效由券商决定，这一条不是「按价必得」"
        )
    if snapshot.margin_enabled:
        lines.append(
            "该账户开启保证金：维持担保比例可能先于本条触发券商处置（该规则本身在 M5）"
        )
    return tuple(lines)


def holding_pnl(snapshot: Snapshot, *, fx: Fx | None = None) -> str:
    """浮动盈亏那一行，两个币种各带自己的标注（§5.4 规则 3；§10.2 最后一条）。

    这里是**标的币种**的浮盈亏，按持仓均价与现价算，不含费用（费用由 `sams ledger` 说）。
    跨币种而没读到汇率时明写「没读到」而不省略：省略的话那一屏上只有标的币种，
    用户会把它当成汇总口径去加总。
    """
    if snapshot.qty is None or snapshot.avg_cost is None or snapshot.price is None:
        return ""
    amount = (snapshot.price - snapshot.avg_cost) * snapshot.qty
    word = "浮盈" if amount >= 0 else "浮亏"
    sign = "" if amount >= 0 else "-"
    base = f"{word} {sign}{abs(amount):,.2f} {snapshot.currency or '元'}"
    report = snapshot.report_currency
    if not report or report == snapshot.currency:
        return base
    if fx is None:
        return (
            f"{base}（汇总币种 {report}：这一趟没读到 {snapshot.currency}→{report} "
            "的汇率，不给折合数）"
        )
    stale = "" if fx.age_days == 0 else f"，{fx.age_days} 天前"
    return (
        f"{base}，折 {fx.currency} {abs(amount) * fx.rate:,.2f}"
        f"（{fx.on} 汇率 {fx.rate:g}{stale}）"
    )
```
