### T-14：`engine.py` + `AlertSender` 端口——一趟求值的完整流水线

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/sams/core/notify.py`（末尾追加 `AlertSender` 那个 Protocol）
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/engine.py`
- Modify: `src/sams/services/rules/__init__.py`（转出名单补全）
- Test: `tests/core/test_notify_alert_sender.py`、`tests/services/test_rules_engine.py`

**这一层要防的三件事，先说清为什么各自住在这一层：**

1. **投递是一个端口，不是一个 import。** 分层守卫写死了 `ALLOWED["monitor"] = {"core", "storage"}`，
   所以 `services/rules/engine.py` 够不着 `NotifyService`。而 `MonitorService` 那一头也够不着引擎
   （它要读 `analysis/valuation`）。中间只留一条缝：`core/notify.py::AlertSender`，`bootstrap` 用四行
   把 `NotifyService.dispatch` 接上去（与 D93 里 `HealthService` 收 `local_status` 同一个形状）。
   端口返回的是 `alerts` 那一行的 **id 串**而不是 `DispatchResult`：后者住在 `monitor`，
   `core` 引用它就是反向依赖。
2. **冷却与去重查真库，不查内存。** §10.1 那句「进程重启从 `alerts` 表恢复冷却状态，不重复推送」
   要求判据是**库里那一行**——内存里那张表一重启就没了，症状是每次重启后半小时里全市场重发一遍。
   所以替身只装在取数（`RuleData`）与投递（`AlertSender`）两个头上。
3. **压制要留话。** §1 那张表第二行的错法就是「冷却把升级吞掉」：屏幕上得有一行「已压制（冷却：
   上一条 hit 在 12:03）」，而不是什么都没有。`sams rule check` 的出口因此不是「发了几条」一个数，
   而是四摞：`raised` / `suppressed` / `skipped`（判不了）/ `blocked`（这条规则本身形状不对），
   外加 `quiet` 一个计数。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/core/test_notify_alert_sender.py`

```python
"""投递端口：形状要在 core，实现要在 monitor，两边各钉一枚。

`AlertSender` 是 `services` 能看到的唯一发送出口，所以它的参数表必须与
`NotifyService.dispatch` 一模一样——差一个参数名，接上去的那一行就会在 M3-D 挂机跑到
某条规则时才炸，而那时红的是网络对面的事。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect

from sams.core.notify import AlertMessage, AlertSender


class _Stub:
    def dispatch(
        self,
        message: AlertMessage,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        channel_ids=(),
        rule_id=None,
        account_id=None,
    ) -> str:
        return "alert-1"


def test_a_stub_with_the_right_shape_satisfies_the_port() -> None:
    assert isinstance(_Stub(), AlertSender)


def test_the_real_router_still_answers_the_port() -> None:
    """参数表逐字对齐（返回类型**应当**不同：那边是 `DispatchResult`，这里是 id 串）。"""
    from sams.monitor.router import NotifyService

    port = inspect.signature(AlertSender.dispatch)
    real = inspect.signature(NotifyService.dispatch)
    assert list(port.parameters) == list(real.parameters)
    assert port.return_annotation is str
```

- [ ] **Step 2：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_engine.py`

这枚文件最长，因为引擎是这一轮唯一把前五层串起来的地方。种子只写「库里有什么」与
「快照是什么」，判据全部走真的 `RuleRepo` / `AlertRepo`。

```python
"""一趟求值：判定 → 过滤 → 冷却 → 投递 → 事件（spec §10.1、D94）。

时钟冻在 2026-10-20T07:30Z（北京 15:30，A 股已收盘）：冷却的判据是
「距上一条多少分钟」，用墙钟的话这枚文件会在跑到第 30 分钟边界时随机红。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta

import pytest

from sams.core.events import AlertTriggered, EventBus, RuleHit
from sams.core.notify import AlertMessage
from sams.storage.repos.alerts import AlertRepo
from sams.storage.repos.fx import FxRepo
from sams.storage.repos.rules import RuleRepo
from sams.services.rules.engine import SESSION_LOOKBACK_DAYS, RuleEngine
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import Snapshot, Target

USER = "u-rule"
MARKET = "A"
CODE = "601088"
#: 北京时间 15:30，A 股已收盘：`price_on` 与「今天」是同一天
NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 20, 7, 30, tzinfo=UTC)
TODAY = NOW.date()


class FixedClock:
    def now_utc(self) -> datetime:
        return NOW

    def today(self) -> date:
        return TODAY


class StubCalendar:
    """日历的替身：只有 10-20 开市，其余日子全闭。时段给空元组 ⇒ `time_window` 不拦。"""

    def __init__(self, *, open_days=(TODAY,)) -> None:
        self.open_days = set(open_days)
        self.day_calls: list[tuple[str, date]] = []

    def is_trading_day(self, market: str, day: date) -> bool:
        self.day_calls.append((market, day))
        return day in self.open_days

    def sessions(self, market: str, day: date) -> tuple:
        return ()


class RecordingSender:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.sent: list[AlertMessage] = []

    def dispatch(
        self,
        message: AlertMessage,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        channel_ids=(),
        rule_id=None,
        account_id=None,
    ) -> str:
        self.sent.append(message)
        return f"alert-{len(self.sent)}"


class StubData:
    """`RuleData` 的替身：引擎只用到 `expand` / `holdings` / `thesis_for` / `read`。"""

    def __init__(self, snapshot: Snapshot, holdings=()) -> None:
        self.snapshot = snapshot
        self.held = list(holdings)

    def expand(self, rule) -> list[Target]:
        return [Target(rule.user_id, MARKET, CODE, rule.account_id, rule.thesis_id)]

    def holdings(self, account_id: str) -> list:
        return list(self.held)

    def thesis_for(self, target: Target):
        return None

    def read(self, target: Target, *, as_of: date, position=None) -> Snapshot:
        return self.snapshot


def snap(**over) -> Snapshot:
    base = {
        "market": MARKET,
        "code": CODE,
        "name": "中国神华",
        "currency": "CNY",
        "report_currency": "CNY",
        "account_name": "SIM-1",
        "price": 9.5,
        "price_on": TODAY,
        "price_age_days": 0,
    }
    base.update(over)
    return Snapshot(**base)


class Harness:
    """一台装好替身的引擎：用例只改「库里有什么」与「快照是什么」。"""

    def __init__(self, db, settings) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.settings = settings
        self.rules = RuleRepo(db, clock=FixedClock())
        self.alerts = AlertRepo(db, clock=FixedClock())
        self.sender = RecordingSender()
        self.hits: list[RuleHit] = []
        self.triggered: list[AlertTriggered] = []
        self.bus = EventBus()
        self.bus.subscribe(RuleHit, self.hits.append)
        self.bus.subscribe(AlertTriggered, self.triggered.append)
        self.calendar = StubCalendar()
        self.snapshot = snap()
        self.held: list = []
        #: `seed_hit` 默认挂在哪条规则上：用例先 `h.add()` 再 `h.seed_hit()` 就对得上
        self.added_id = "r-seed"

    def engine(self) -> RuleEngine:
        return RuleEngine(
            rules=self.rules,
            alerts=self.alerts,
            data=StubData(self.snapshot, self.held),
            calendar=self.calendar,
            fx=FxRepo(self.db),
            clock=FixedClock(),
            bus=self.bus,
            sender=self.sender,
            settings=self.settings,
        )

    def add(self, **over):
        kw = {
            "kind": "stop.fixed",
            "name": "跌破 10 元",
            "target_type": "symbol",
            "target_ref": f"{MARKET}/{CODE}",
            "params": {"price": 10.0},
        }
        kw.update(over)
        return self.rules.create(USER, **kw)

    def seed_hit(self, *, minutes_ago=0, stage="hit", rule_id=None, account_id=None):
        """在去重键上种一条**已经推过**的告警：冷却与 `no_open_alert` 的输入。"""
        return self.alerts.create(
            USER,
            rule_id=rule_id or self.added_id,
            rule_name="跌破 10 元",
            account_id=account_id,
            account_name="SIM-1",
            market=MARKET,
            code=CODE,
            symbol_name="中国神华",
            severity="strong",
            stage=stage,
            value_text="9.50 CNY",
            threshold_text="10.00 CNY",
            action_note="止损位到了",
            triggered_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=minutes_ago),
        )


@pytest.fixture
def h(db, settings) -> Harness:
    return Harness(db, settings)


# --- 投递那一条路 ------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_a_breach_raises_one_alert_and_two_events(h) -> None:
    h.add()
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert report.quiet == 0
    assert len(h.sender.sent) == 1
    message = h.sender.sent[0]
    assert message.severity == "strong"
    assert message.stage == "hit"
    assert message.value_text == "9.50 CNY"
    assert message.threshold_text == "10.00 CNY"
    assert report.raised[0].alert_id == "alert-1"
    assert [hit.stage for hit in h.hits] == ["hit"]
    assert [got.alert_id for got in h.triggered] == ["alert-1"]


def test_the_action_line_names_the_kind_and_the_users_own_name(h) -> None:
    h.add(name="别让它跌过十块")
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    note = h.sender.sent[0].action_note
    assert "止损位到了" in note and "别让它跌过十块" in note
    assert report.raised[0].action_note == note


def test_the_body_carries_the_pnl_the_constraint_and_the_reason(h) -> None:
    h.add()
    h.snapshot = snap(qty=1000.0, avg_cost=12.0, opened_on=TODAY)
    h.engine().check(USER)
    body = h.sender.sent[0].body
    assert "浮亏 2,500.00 CNY" in body
    assert "T+1" in body  # 今日买入：这条今天执行不了
    assert "绝对价止损线 10.00 CNY" in body


def test_an_above_line_price_leaves_the_run_quiet(h) -> None:
    h.add()
    h.snapshot = snap(price=11.0)
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert report.raised == () and report.suppressed == ()
    assert report.skipped == () and report.quiet == 1


def test_a_missing_price_is_reported_as_unable_not_as_quiet(h) -> None:
    """「判不了」与「没到」是两句话（§1 的取向：判不准就放行，但要说）。"""
    h.add()
    h.snapshot = snap(price=None, price_on=None, price_age_days=None)
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert report.raised == () and report.quiet == 0
    assert len(report.skipped) == 1
    assert "没有" in report.skipped[0].reason and "现价" in report.skipped[0].reason


def test_a_dry_run_judges_everything_and_sends_nothing(h) -> None:
    h.added_id = h.add().id
    report = h.engine().check(USER, dry_run=True)
    assert report.dry_run is True
    assert len(report.raised) == 1
    assert report.raised[0].alert_id == ""
    assert h.sender.sent == [] and h.hits == [] and h.triggered == []


# --- 冷却（D94）--------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_cooldown_swallows_a_repeat_of_the_same_stage(h) -> None:
    h.added_id = h.add().id
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=5, stage="hit")
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert h.sender.sent == []
    assert len(report.suppressed) == 1
    why = report.suppressed[0].reason
    assert "冷却" in why and "上一条" in why and "hit" in why


def test_an_escalation_punches_through_the_cooldown(h) -> None:
    """D94：`pre` 报过之后 30 分钟内的 `hit` 是真跌破，不能被降噪吞掉。"""
    h.added_id = h.add().id
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=5, stage="pre")
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert len(h.sender.sent) == 1
    assert report.suppressed == () and report.raised[0].stage == "hit"


def test_a_hit_beyond_the_window_reports_again(h) -> None:
    h.added_id = h.add().id
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=31, stage="hit")
    assert len(h.engine().check(USER).raised) == 1


def test_the_cooldown_window_is_the_rules_own_number(h) -> None:
    """`cooldown_min = 0` 的意思是「每一次都报」（§10.1 那个默认值不是命令）。"""
    h.added_id = h.add(cooldown_min=0).id
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=0, stage="hit")
    assert len(h.engine().check(USER).raised) == 1


def test_another_accounts_hit_does_not_silence_this_one(h) -> None:
    """§1 那张表的第三行：去重键含 `account_id`（§10.1）。

    两头都钉：A 账户那条拦不住 B 账户的破位；同一条 B 的才拦得住。
    只钉前一头的话，把键写成「不含 account_id」也能过——因为那时谁也拦不住谁。
    """
    h.added_id = h.add(account_id="acc-b").id
    h.snapshot = snap(account_id="acc-b")
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=5, stage="hit", account_id="acc-a")
    assert len(h.engine().check(USER).raised) == 1
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=5, stage="hit", account_id="acc-b")
    assert h.engine().check(USER).suppressed != ()


# --- 四个开关在这一趟里的位置（§10.1）------------------------------------------------------

def test_a_switch_suppresses_and_says_which_one(h) -> None:
    h.added_id = h.add(filters={"data_freshness": True}).id
    h.snapshot = snap(price_age_days=10)
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert h.sender.sent == []
    assert "10 天前" in report.suppressed[0].reason


def test_no_open_alert_suppresses_a_quieter_repeat_on_the_same_symbol(h) -> None:
    h.added_id = h.add(filters={"no_open_alert": True}).id
    h.seed_hit(minutes_ago=5, stage="urgent")
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert "未处理" in report.suppressed[0].reason


def test_the_gate_asks_the_calendar_for_the_targets_own_market(h) -> None:
    h.add()
    h.engine().check(USER)
    assert h.calendar.day_calls[0][0] == MARKET


def test_the_last_session_is_walked_back_from_the_local_day(h) -> None:
    """日历一个交易日都没给 ⇒ `exclude_halt` 不拦（判不了就放行），这里钉它确实回走过。"""
    h.add()
    h.calendar = StubCalendar(open_days=())  # 日历说这些天全闭
    h.engine().check(USER)
    assert len(h.calendar.day_calls) >= SESSION_LOOKBACK_DAYS


# --- 一趟的形状：一条坏规则不拖整列（§1 第五行）--------------------------------------------

def test_a_rule_that_cannot_expand_blocks_only_itself(h) -> None:
    good = h.add(name="好的那条")
    # 绕过仓库直接写一行「持仓类却没有账户」：仓库那道闸拦的是写入，这一枚要的是库里
    # 已经躺着这种行时（M3-D 的派生写入方、手工修过库）一趟照旧出别的面
    h.db.execute(
        "INSERT INTO rules (id, user_id, name, enabled, target_type, target_ref,"
        " kind, condition_kind, params_json, filters_json, cooldown_min, severity,"
        " channel_ids, created_at, updated_at)"
        " VALUES ('bad-row', ?, '缺账户的那条', TRUE, 'position', '*', 'stop.pct',"
        " 'template', '{\"pct\": 0.08}', '{}', 30, 'strong', '[]', ?, ?)",
        [USER, NOW, NOW],
    )
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert [got.rule_id for got in report.raised] == [good.id]
    assert len(report.blocked) == 1
    assert "account_id" in report.blocked[0].reason


def test_disabled_rules_are_skipped_unless_named(h) -> None:
    made = h.add(enabled=False)
    assert h.engine().check(USER).raised == ()
    assert len(h.engine().check(USER, rule_id=made.id).raised) == 1


def test_as_of_moves_the_reading_day_without_moving_the_clock(h) -> None:
    h.added_id = h.add().id
    report = h.engine().check(USER, as_of=date(2026, 10, 16))
    assert report.as_of == date(2026, 10, 16)
    assert len(report.raised) == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/core/test_notify_alert_sender.py tests/services/test_rules_engine.py -q`
Expected: `ImportError: cannot import name 'AlertSender'`（第一枚）、`ModuleNotFoundError: sams.services.rules.engine`（第二枚）。

- [ ] **Step 4：写端口** —— `src/sams/core/notify.py`（末尾追加；文件头的 import 补
  `from collections.abc import Sequence` 与 `from typing import Protocol, runtime_checkable`）

```python
@runtime_checkable
class AlertSender(Protocol):
    """规则引擎唯一的发送出口（D95）。`services` 不许 import `monitor`，而投递必须发生。

    返回的是 `alerts` 那一行的 **id**，不是 `DispatchResult`：那个结果类型住在
    `monitor/router.py`，`core` 引用它就是反向依赖。引擎拿 id 做两件 `monitor` 之外也能做的
    事——发 `AlertTriggered`（M3-D 的调度按它数当日已推送）与把 id 报回屏幕。
    `bootstrap` 用四行把 `NotifyService.dispatch` 的 `DispatchResult.alert_id` 折成这个串。

    参数表与 `NotifyService.dispatch` 逐字对齐（`tests/core/test_notify_alert_sender.py`
    钉这一条）：差一个参数名，红的是 M3-D 挂机跑到某条规则时，而不是这里。
    """

    def dispatch(
        self,
        message: AlertMessage,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        channel_ids: Sequence[str] = (),
        rule_id: str | None = None,
        account_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> str: ...
```

- [ ] **Step 5：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/engine.py`

```python
"""一趟求值：判定 → 过滤 → 冷却 → 投递 → 事件（spec §10.1、D94、D95）。

两处顺序不能调：

- **过滤在冷却之前**。冷却问的是「这条去重键上最近一次命中是什么时候」，而被
  「开盘 5 分钟内」拦下的那次读数根本没走到「命中」这一步。让它先排队，冷却窗口就会被
  一堆没推出去的读数填满——真破位那次正好落在窗口里，于是没人听见（§1 那张表第二行）。
- **事件在投递之后**。`RuleHit` / `AlertTriggered` 只在真投递的那条路径上发：
  M3-D 的调度与 M4 的忽略率统计读的都是「发出去过几条」，压制与静默不发事件。

冷却与去重**直接查 `alerts`**：§10.1 那句「进程重启从 `alerts` 表恢复冷却状态」要求判据
是库里那一行，内存里那张表一重启就没了，症状是每次重启后半小时全市场重发一遍。
所以这一层的替身只装在取数（`RuleData`）与投递（`AlertSender`）两个头上。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from sams.core.clock import SYSTEM_CLOCK, Clock
from sams.core.config import Settings
from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.core.events import AlertTriggered, EventBus, RuleHit
from sams.core.notify import AlertMessage, AlertSender
from sams.core.rules import Verdict, escalates, template_for
from sams.storage.repos.alerts import AlertRepo
from sams.storage.repos.fx import FxRepo
from sams.storage.repos.ledger import Position
from sams.storage.repos.rules import Rule, RuleRepo
from sams.services.rules.filters import Gate, OpenAlert, run_filters
from sams.services.rules.notes import Fx, constraint_lines, holding_pnl
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import RuleData, Snapshot, Target
from sams.services.rules.templates import Ask, TABLE

#: 往回找「最近一个已开市的日子」最多找这么多天。20 天盖住春节与国庆两段连休，
#: 再长就该由日历与健康页说话，而不是让引擎拿一个猜出来的日子去判停牌。
SESSION_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 20

#: 逐个 kind 的那几句：家族说不出「复核」与「清仓」的差别
ACTIONS: dict[str, str] = {
    "stop.time": "复核这笔：持有到期而没达预期",
    "stop.thesis_drift": "先复核理由，再决定这笔的去留",
    "tp.tiered": "按分档计划处置这笔的一部分",
    "scheduled.reevaluate": "该重读这条论了：新的一期已披露",
}

#: 家族级：同一家族的动作是同一句话。写成家族而不是九个 kind，是为了让下一次
#: 加止损模板的人不必记得补——漏补的症状只是屏幕上退回那句通用的话。
_FAMILY = {
    "stop": "止损位到了，按处置计划动这笔",
    "price": "价位到了，看一眼这笔要不要动",
    "thesis": "这条论变了，复核它的价位与赔率还成不成立",
}

DEFAULT_ACTION = "按这条规则处置"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Raised:
    """一条真的推出去的告警。`alert_id` 是库里那一行，`sams alert show` 按它追全过程。"""

    alert_id: str
    rule_id: str
    rule_name: str
    user_id: str
    market: str
    code: str
    name: str
    account_id: str | None
    stage: str
    severity: str
    value_text: str
    threshold_text: str
    action_note: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Suppressed:
    """判到了、这一趟不推。`reason` 是要上屏幕的那句话——沉默是这一层最贵的错法。"""

    rule_id: str
    rule_name: str
    market: str
    code: str
    stage: str
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Skipped:
    """判不了：模板缺哪一路输入就说哪一路（与「没到」分开计数）。"""

    rule_id: str
    rule_name: str
    market: str
    code: str
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Blocked:
    """这条规则本身的形状不对（展开失败），一趟里只它停，别的照跑。"""

    rule_id: str
    rule_name: str
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunReport:
    """一趟的全部出口。`sams rule check` 的四摞与 `--json` 的四个数组读同一份。"""

    as_of: date
    now: datetime
    dry_run: bool
    rules: int
    raised: tuple[Raised, ...] = ()
    suppressed: tuple[Suppressed, ...] = ()
    skipped: tuple[Skipped, ...] = ()
    blocked: tuple[Blocked, ...] = ()
    quiet: int = 0

    @property
    def judged(self) -> int:
        """到了「判」这一步的对象数：不含展开失败的那几条规则。"""
        return len(self.raised) + len(self.suppressed) + len(self.skipped) + self.quiet


def action_for(kind: str, name: str) -> str:
    """§11 标题行里那句「要做什么」：动词按 kind 定，尾巴挂用户自己起的名。"""
    family = kind.split(".", 1)[0]
    head = ACTIONS.get(kind) or _FAMILY.get(family) or DEFAULT_ACTION
    return f"{head}（{name}）"


@dataclass
class _Cache:
    """一趟里的三张临时表。它们只在一趟内有效：跨趟复用持仓会把「今天新买的那笔」漏掉。"""

    positions: dict[str, dict[tuple[str, str], Position]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    sessions: dict[tuple[str, date], tuple] = field(default_factory=dict)
    last_session: dict[tuple[str, date], date | None] = field(default_factory=dict)


class RuleEngine:
    """把一条规则跑成一个对象的一次判定。构造参数全是 `Container` 上已有的字段。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        rules: RuleRepo,
        alerts: AlertRepo,
        data: RuleData,
        calendar,
        fx: FxRepo,
        bus: EventBus,
        sender: AlertSender,
        settings: Settings,
        clock: Clock = SYSTEM_CLOCK,
    ) -> None:
        self.rules = rules
        self.alerts = alerts
        self.data = data
        self.calendar = calendar
        self.fx = fx
        self.bus = bus
        self.sender = sender
        self.settings = settings
        self.clock = clock

    # --- 出口 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def check(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        *,
        as_of: date | None = None,
        dry_run: bool = False,
        rule_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> RunReport:
        """一趟同步求值。M3-D 的调度器以后按市场时段调这里，本函数不知道自己被调了几次。"""
        now = self.clock.now_utc()
        day = as_of if as_of is not None else self.clock.today()
        raised: list[Raised] = []
        suppressed: list[Suppressed] = []
        skipped: list[Skipped] = []
        blocked: list[Blocked] = []
        quiet = 0
        cache = _Cache()
        selected = self._select(user_id, rule_id)
        for rule in selected:
            try:
                targets = self.data.expand(rule)
            except ValidationError as exc:
                blocked.append(Blocked(rule_id=rule.id, rule_name=rule.name, reason=str(exc)))
                continue
            held = self._holdings(rule, cache)
            for target in targets:
                fate = self._one(
                    rule,
                    target,
                    position=held.get((target.market, target.code)),
                    day=day,
                    now=now,
                    dry_run=dry_run,
                    cache=cache,
                )
                if isinstance(fate, Raised):
                    raised.append(fate)
                elif isinstance(fate, Suppressed):
                    suppressed.append(fate)
                elif isinstance(fate, Skipped):
                    skipped.append(fate)
                else:
                    quiet += 1
        return RunReport(
            as_of=day,
            now=now,
            dry_run=dry_run,
            rules=len(selected),
            raised=tuple(raised),
            suppressed=tuple(suppressed),
            skipped=tuple(skipped),
            blocked=tuple(blocked),
            quiet=quiet,
        )

    def _select(self, user_id: str, rule_id: str | None) -> list[Rule]:
        """点名一条规则时绕过 `enabled`：用户刚停用一条又 `--rule` 点它，
        要的显然是「这条如果开着会判出什么」，回一句空列表最没用。"""
        listed = self.rules.list_for(user_id, enabled_only=rule_id is None)
        if rule_id is None:
            return listed
        return [rule for rule in listed if rule.id == rule_id]

    # --- 一个对象 -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _one(
        self,
        rule: Rule,
        target: Target,
        *,
        position: Position | None,
        day: date,
        now: datetime,
        dry_run: bool,
        cache: _Cache,
    ) -> Raised | Suppressed | Skipped | None:
        built = template_for(rule.kind)
        snapshot = self.data.read(target, as_of=day, position=position)
        verdict = self._verdict(rule, target, snapshot)
        if isinstance(verdict, Skipped):
            return verdict
        if verdict is None:
            return None  # 判过了，没到
        reason = run_filters(
            rule.filters,
            snapshot=snapshot,
            gate=self._gate(rule, target, snapshot, now, cache),
            verdict=verdict,
            built=built,
        )
        if reason:
            return self._suppressed(rule, snapshot, verdict, reason)
        cooled = self._cooldown(rule, snapshot, verdict, now)
        if cooled:
            return self._suppressed(rule, snapshot, verdict, cooled)
        if dry_run:
            return self._raised("", rule, snapshot, verdict, now)
        return self._dispatch(rule, snapshot, verdict, built, now)

    def _verdict(
        self, rule: Rule, target: Target, snapshot: Snapshot
    ) -> Verdict | Skipped | None:
        """查表、求值。表里没有这个 kind ⇒ 判不了：仓库那道闸拦的是写入，
        这一头是第二道——库里已经躺着的那种行不该把整趟拖崩。

        `thesis` 从引擎这里递进去而不是让模板自己去查库：`data.read` 已经查过一次，
        两次之间规则还能被改，那样算出来的价位与屏幕上那一条不是同一个数（`Ask` 的注释）。
        """
        evaluate = TABLE.get(rule.kind)
        if evaluate is None:
            return Skipped(
                rule_id=rule.id,
                rule_name=rule.name,
                market=snapshot.market,
                code=snapshot.code,
                reason=f"这一层没有 {rule.kind} 的求值实现：名单的唯一出处是 core/rules.py",
            )
        thesis = self.data.thesis_for(target)
        outcome = evaluate(Ask(snapshot=snapshot, params=rule.params, thesis=thesis))
        if outcome.hit is not None:
            return outcome.hit
        if outcome.why_not:
            return Skipped(
                rule_id=rule.id,
                rule_name=rule.name,
                market=snapshot.market,
                code=snapshot.code,
                reason=outcome.why_not,
            )
        return None

    # --- 闸门（§10.1）--------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _gate(
        self, rule: Rule, target: Target, snapshot: Snapshot, now: datetime, cache: _Cache
    ) -> Gate:
        """四开关的输入。本地日按**标的市场**的时区算：拿 UTC 日推美股时段恒错一天。"""
        day = self._local_day(target.market, now)
        if day is None:
            return Gate(now=now)  # 市场不在配置里 ⇒ 三个要日历的开关都读不到输入
        sessions = self._sessions(target.market, day, cache)
        open_alert = self.alerts.latest_open(rule.user_id, snapshot.market, snapshot.code)
        return Gate(
            now=now,
            sessions=sessions,
            last_session_on=self._last_session(target.market, day, cache),
            open_alert=(
                None
                if open_alert is None
                else OpenAlert(
                    open_alert.id, open_alert.stage, open_alert.triggered_at.date()
                )
            ),
        )

    def _local_day(self, market: str, now: datetime) -> date | None:
        try:
            zone = ZoneInfo(self.settings.market_cfg(market).tz)
        except ValidationError:
            return None
        return now.astimezone(zone).date()

    def _sessions(self, market: str, day: date, cache: _Cache) -> tuple:
        key = (market, day)
        if key not in cache.sessions:
            cache.sessions[key] = tuple(self.calendar.sessions(market, day))
        return cache.sessions[key]

    def _last_session(self, market: str, day: date, cache: _Cache) -> date | None:
        key = (market, day)
        if key in cache.last_session:
            return cache.last_session[key]
        found: date | None = None
        probe = day
        for _ in range(SESSION_LOOKBACK_DAYS):
            if self.calendar.is_trading_day(market, probe):
                found = probe
                break
            probe -= timedelta(days=1)
        cache.last_session[key] = found
        return found

    # --- 冷却（D94）---------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _cooldown(
        self, rule: Rule, snapshot: Snapshot, verdict: Verdict, now: datetime
    ) -> str:
        last = self.alerts.latest_for(
            rule.id, snapshot.market, snapshot.code, account_id=snapshot.account_id
        )
        if last is None:
            return ""
        if escalates(last.stage, verdict.stage):
            return ""  # 更响的那一档：止损真破了，降噪不能替它做决定
        gap = now - last.triggered_at
        window = timedelta(minutes=rule.cooldown_min)
        if gap >= window:
            return ""
        return (
            f"冷却：上一条 {last.stage} 推出于 {last.triggered_at:%m-%d %H:%M} UTC，"
            f"距今 {int(gap.total_seconds() // 60)} 分钟，窗口 {rule.cooldown_min} 分钟；"
            f"这一条 {verdict.stage} 不比它更响"
        )

    # --- 投递 ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _dispatch(
        self, rule: Rule, snapshot: Snapshot, verdict: Verdict, built, now: datetime
    ) -> Raised:
        message = self._message(rule, snapshot, verdict, built, now)
        alert_id = self.sender.dispatch(
            message,
            user_id=rule.user_id,
            channel_ids=rule.channel_ids,
            rule_id=rule.id,
            account_id=snapshot.account_id,
        )
        self.bus.publish(
            RuleHit(
                rule_id=rule.id,
                user_id=rule.user_id,
                market=snapshot.market,
                code=snapshot.code,
                stage=verdict.stage,
                account_id=snapshot.account_id,
                payload={
                    "kind": rule.kind,
                    "value": verdict.value,
                    "threshold": verdict.threshold,
                    "value_text": verdict.value_text,
                    "threshold_text": verdict.threshold_text,
                },
                ts=now,
            )
        )
        self.bus.publish(
            AlertTriggered(
                alert_id=alert_id, rule_id=rule.id, user_id=rule.user_id, ts=now
            )
        )
        return self._raised(alert_id, rule, snapshot, verdict, now)

    def _raised(
        self, alert_id: str, rule: Rule, snapshot: Snapshot, verdict: Verdict, now
    ) -> Raised:
        return Raised(
            alert_id=alert_id,
            rule_id=rule.id,
            rule_name=rule.name,
            user_id=rule.user_id,
            market=snapshot.market,
            code=snapshot.code,
            name=snapshot.name,
            account_id=snapshot.account_id,
            stage=verdict.stage,
            severity=rule.severity,
            value_text=verdict.value_text,
            threshold_text=verdict.threshold_text,
            action_note=action_for(rule.kind, rule.name),
        )

    def _suppressed(
        self, rule: Rule, snapshot: Snapshot, verdict: Verdict, reason: str
    ) -> Suppressed:
        return Suppressed(
            rule_id=rule.id,
            rule_name=rule.name,
            market=snapshot.market,
            code=snapshot.code,
            stage=verdict.stage,
            reason=reason,
        )

    def _message(
        self, rule: Rule, snapshot: Snapshot, verdict: Verdict, built, now: datetime
    ) -> AlertMessage:
        return AlertMessage(
            market=snapshot.market,
            code=snapshot.code,
            severity=rule.severity,
            triggered_at=now,
            name=snapshot.name,
            account_name=snapshot.account_name,
            rule_name=rule.name,
            value_text=verdict.value_text,
            threshold_text=verdict.threshold_text,
            action_note=action_for(rule.kind, rule.name),
            stage=verdict.stage,
            body=self._body(rule, built, snapshot, verdict),
        )

    def _body(self, rule: Rule, built, snapshot: Snapshot, verdict: Verdict) -> str:
        """正文的四摞，顺序就是读的顺序：线在哪 → 这笔现在什么样 → 今天能不能执行 → 缺什么。

        「能不能执行」放在盈亏之后（§10.2 要求每条告警都带）：先让人知道这笔亏多少，
        再说这一笔今天动不了——反过来的话，读到第二句的人以为自己已经没有损失可言。
        """
        lines = [f"{built.label}：{verdict.why}" if verdict.why else built.label]
        pnl = holding_pnl(snapshot, fx=self._fx(snapshot))
        if pnl:
            lines.append(pnl)
        lines.extend(constraint_lines(snapshot))
        if snapshot.missing:
            lines.append("缺的输入：" + "、".join(snapshot.missing))
        lines.append(f"规则 `{rule.name}`（{rule.kind}）· 去重键 {rule.id}/{snapshot.market}/"
                     f"{snapshot.code}/{snapshot.account_id or '-'}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def _fx(self, snapshot: Snapshot) -> Fx | None:
        """汇总币种那一边的汇率。同币种不需要，跨币种读不到就说读不到（`holding_pnl` 那句）。"""
        base = snapshot.currency
        report = snapshot.report_currency
        if not base or not report or base == report:
            return None
        got = self.fx.latest(self.clock.today(), base, report)
        if got is None:
            return None
        return Fx(currency=got.quote, rate=got.rate, on=got.day, age_days=got.age_days)

    def _holdings(self, rule: Rule, cache: _Cache) -> dict[tuple[str, str], Position]:
        """一趟里同一个账户只读一次。

        读两次的代价不是性能：两次之间若有一笔成交进来，第二次拿到的成本与第一次不是
        同一个数，而告警里那两个数会同时出现（`tp.tiered` 用均价、`holding_pnl` 也用均价）。
        """
        account_id = rule.account_id
        if account_id is None:
            return {}
        if account_id not in cache.positions:
            cache.positions[account_id] = {
                (row.market, row.code): row for row in self.data.holdings(account_id)
            }
        return cache.positions[account_id]
```

`RuleHit` 的 `payload` 带数值也带文本，与 `Verdict` 同一个理由（T-1）：M4 的统计要按数值
分桶，而 `sams alerts` 那一屏要按文本显示——只给一头的另一头就会去 parse 另一个。

- [ ] **Step 6：`__init__.py` 补全转出名单**

```python
"""规则引擎（spec §10）：取数、模板、过滤、执行约束、一趟求值。

放在 `services` 而不是 `monitor` 是分层守卫决定的：引擎要读 `analysis/valuation/portfolio`
那些服务，而 `ALLOWED["monitor"] = {"core", "storage"}`。投递因此是一个注入进来的端口
（`core/notify.py::AlertSender`，T-14），`bootstrap` 把 `NotifyService.dispatch` 接上去——
与 D93 里 `HealthService` 收 `local_status` 同一个形状。
"""

from sams.services.rules.engine import (
    Blocked,
    Raised,
    RunReport,
    RuleEngine,
    Skipped,
    Suppressed,
    action_for,
)
from sams.services.rules.filters import FILTERS, Gate, OpenAlert, run_filters
from sams.services.rules.notes import Fx, constraint_lines, holding_pnl
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import RuleData, Snapshot, Target
from sams.services.rules.templates import Ask, Outcome, TABLE

__all__ = [
    "Ask",
    "Blocked",
    "FILTERS",
    "Fx",
    "Gate",
    "OpenAlert",
    "Outcome",
    "Raised",
    "RuleData",
    "RuleEngine",
    "RunReport",
    "Skipped",
    "Snapshot",
    "Suppressed",
    "TABLE",
    "Target",
    "action_for",
    "constraint_lines",
    "holding_pnl",
    "run_filters",
]
```

`__all__` 里之所以有二十来个名字：T-17 的 `RuleService` 与 T-18 的 CLI 都从
`sams.services.rules` 这一个口取，而它们不该知道 `services.rules.engine` 这种模块路径。

- [ ] **Step 6.5：把端口接到 `bootstrap` 上（这一段是接线，用例在 T-18 的 CLI 那一头）**

`src/sams/bootstrap.py`：`Container` 多三个字段——`rules_store: RuleRepo`（与
`theses_store` / `fx_store` 同一个命名法，`rules` 那个名字留给 T-17 的服务）、
`rule_data: RuleData`、`rule_engine: RuleEngine`。投递端口这样接：

```python
class _NotifyAlertSender:
    """把 `NotifyService.dispatch` 的 `DispatchResult` 折成 id 串（D95）。

    只有四行的适配器不是多余的一层：`services` 不许 import `monitor`，而
    `DispatchResult` 住在那里。引擎要的只有 `alert_id`，别的那些（每条通道的
    `DeliveryReport`）留在 `monitor` 那一头，由 `sams alert show` 去查。
    """

    def __init__(self, notify: NotifyService) -> None:
        self._notify = notify

    def dispatch(
        self,
        message: AlertMessage,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        channel_ids: Sequence[str] = (),
        rule_id: str | None = None,
        account_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        return self._notify.dispatch(
            message,
            user_id=user_id,
            channel_ids=channel_ids,
            rule_id=rule_id,
            account_id=account_id,
        ).alert_id
```

`Sequence` 从 `collections.abc` 引（`bootstrap` 已经引过 `Mapping` 那一类）。构造顺序照
`Container` 上现有的写法：`rule_data` 先于 `rule_engine`，因为后者收前者。`bootstrap`
允许 import 任何层（分层守卫里它不被检查），所以这一头不需要端口。

- [ ] **Step 6.6：读不到对象时别把整趟拖崩**

`RuleData.read` 在「库里一根 K 线都没有、主档里也没这个代码」时抛 `NotAdjustable`
（M2-B 那一句「代码多半写错了」）。裸调它的后果不是那条规则停，是**整趟**打成一次异常：
屏幕上什么都不留，同一趟里别的规则本来该报的破位也跟着没了。这正是 `Skipped` 那一类
（判不了，并且说得出为什么），所以补在这里而不是 T-18 的 CLI 那一头——命令行不该替
引擎兜形状。

`src/sams/services/rules/engine.py` 的 `_one` 开头那两行换成：

```python
        try:
            snapshot = self.data.read(target, as_of=day, position=position)
        except NotAdjustable as exc:
            return Skipped(
                rule_id=rule.id,
                rule_name=rule.name,
                market=target.market,
                code=target.code,
                reason=f"读不到这个对象：{exc}",
            )
        verdict = self._verdict(rule, target, snapshot)
```

模块那行 import 跟着补：`from sams.core.errors import NotAdjustable, ValidationError`。
只兜 `NotAdjustable` 这一种，不兜 `SamsError`：后者会把「取数那条链上有一个真 bug」
也折成屏幕上的一句 skipped，而那一趟看起来是成功的。

替身那头要能装出这个异常。`tests/services/test_rules_engine.py` 里 `StubData` 加一个
后门，`Harness` 把它接到 `engine()` 上（`StubData` 的 `held` 是 `list(...)` 拷出来的，
所以它必须继续在 `engine()` 里构造，挪到 `__init__` 会让用例先改 `h.held` 再拿旧拷贝）：

```python
class StubData:
    """`RuleData` 的替身：引擎只用到 `expand` / `holdings` / `thesis_for` / `read`。"""

    def __init__(self, snapshot: Snapshot, holdings=()) -> None:
        self.snapshot = snapshot
        self.held = list(holdings)
        #: 装上它，`read` 就抛：一条挂了错代码的规则不该把整趟拖崩
        self.read_raises: Exception | None = None

    def read(self, target: Target, *, as_of: date, position=None) -> Snapshot:
        if self.read_raises is not None:
            raise self.read_raises
        return self.snapshot
```

`Harness.__init__` 里 `self.held: list = []` 那行之后加 `self.data: StubData | None = None`，
`engine()` 改成先建替身再用它：

```python
    def engine(self) -> RuleEngine:
        data = StubData(self.snapshot, self.held)
        data.read_raises = self.read_raises
        self.data = data
        return RuleEngine(
            rules=self.rules,
            alerts=self.alerts,
            data=data,
            calendar=self.calendar,
            fx=FxRepo(self.db),
            clock=FixedClock(),
            bus=self.bus,
            sender=self.sender,
            settings=self.settings,
        )
```

（`Harness.__init__` 跟着多一行 `self.read_raises: Exception | None = None`。）

用例加在 `test_a_dry_run_judges_everything_and_sends_nothing` 之后：

```python
def test_an_unreadable_target_is_skipped_instead_of_killing_the_run(h) -> None:
    """挂在一个查无此码上的规则：这一条判不了，那一趟要跑完并且把原因写出来。"""
    h.read_raises = NotAdjustable("A/000001 全库一根 K 线都没有，主档里也没有这个代码")
    h.add(target_ref="A/000001")
    report = h.engine().check(USER)
    assert report.rules == 1 and report.judged == 1
    assert len(report.skipped) == 1 and report.raised == ()
    assert "K 线" in report.skipped[0].reason
    assert h.sender.sent == []
```

（放在 `import pytest` 之后那一段，`sams.*` 之前）。

### T-15：`derive.py`——§9.2 的六条派生规则与「改参数回写哪一列」

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/derive.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_derive.py`

**§9.2 那六行落到这一层时要做掉的四个决定，先写清：**

1. **「达到目标价」那一行写的 kind 是 `price.cross_above`，实现取 `tp.tiered`。** 那一行的
   说明列写的是「分批止盈提醒」，而 §10.2 里唯一能报「到了半程」与「到了目标」两档的就是
   `tp.tiered`；用 `price.cross_above` 就得把 `target_price` 抄进 `params`，抄了就要在两处同步，
   而 §9.2 只规定了一个同步方向（规则 → 论）。`tp.tiered` 走 `Ask.thesis` 读那一列，不抄数。
2. **只有两条派生规则抄了论上的数字**（`stop.fixed` 抄 `stop_price`、`price.cross_below` 抄
   `entry_high`），所以回写表只有两项。剩下四条读的是论本身或日历，改了论不必动它们。
3. **回写只发生在「挂着 `thesis_id`」的规则上。** 用户自己建的一条 `price.cross_below` 里那个
   数是他心里的价，不是他论点上的区间上沿——回写会把一条与当前论点无关的东西写进论点。
   这一条与 `RuleRepo.remove` 的「`thesis_id` 非空不可删除」是同一个判据的两个方向。
4. **`tiers` 进 `params` 时是比例串（`"0.5,1.0"`），不是列表。** 模板表把这一族的形状定成
   `shape="tiers"`，而 `_as_tiers` 明确拒收数字（列表会被 `str()` 打出方括号，然后在「不是
   数字」上炸）。库里的 `params_json` 存的是校验后的 `list[float]`——写进去用串、读出来是
   列表，这一条在 Step 1 的两枚用例里各测一次，别只测一边。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_derive.py`

```python
"""§9.2：一条论 → 六条规则。派生要能重复跑，回写要能指名道姓。

仓库层全用真的：`by_thesis` 与 `remove` 那两道闸就是这一枚文件的验收线，
把它们 stub 掉等于把「不可删除，只能停用」那句话删掉。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.services.rules.derive import (
    ODDS_FLOOR,
    TIERS,
    NotDerived,
    RuleDeriver,
    blueprints_for,
    write_back,
)
from sams.storage.repos.rules import RuleRepo
from sams.storage.repos.theses import ThesisRepo

USER = "u-derive"
T0 = datetime(2026, 10, 20, 1, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
LEVELS = {
    "entry_low": 32.0,
    "entry_high": 36.0,
    "stop_price": 28.0,
    "target_price": 52.0,
    "reward_risk": 2.0,
}


@pytest.fixture
def theses(db) -> ThesisRepo:
    return ThesisRepo(db)


@pytest.fixture
def rules(db) -> RuleRepo:
    return RuleRepo(db)


def make_thesis(theses, **over):
    levels = dict(LEVELS)
    levels.update(over)
    return theses.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="601088",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status="proposed",
        **{k: v for k, v in levels.items() if v is not None},
    )


def skip_reasons(got):
    """这次没建的那些，连它们各自的理由。"""
    return {item.kind: item.reason for item in got if isinstance(item, NotDerived)}


def test_six_blueprints_when_the_thesis_is_complete(theses) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    got = blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")
    assert len(got) == 6
    assert {item.kind for item in got} == {
        "price.cross_below",
        "stop.fixed",
        "tp.tiered",
        "thesis.odds_below",
        "scheduled.reevaluate",
        "thesis.drift",
    }
    assert skip_reasons(got) == {}


def test_every_blueprint_names_which_column_it_came_from(theses) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    by_kind = {item.kind: item for item in blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")}
    assert "36" in by_kind["price.cross_below"].reason
    assert "28" in by_kind["stop.fixed"].reason
    assert "52" in by_kind["tp.tiered"].reason
    assert by_kind["tp.tiered"].params["tiers"] == TIERS
    assert by_kind["thesis.odds_below"].params["threshold"] == ODDS_FLOOR


def test_a_missing_target_skips_the_tier_only(theses) -> None:
    """缺哪一列就少哪一条，并且说得出为什么——不是六条悄悄变成五条。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses, target_price=None)
    got = blueprints_for(thesis, account_id="acc-1")
    missing = skip_reasons(got)
    assert set(missing) == {"tp.tiered"}
    assert "目标价" in missing["tp.tiered"]
    assert sum(1 for item in got if not isinstance(item, NotDerived)) == 5


def test_a_tier_without_an_account_is_skipped_with_a_named_reason(theses) -> None:
    """`tp.tiered` 读的是这笔的成本：没有账户就没有成本，硬建出来是一条永远不响的规则。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    missing = skip_reasons(blueprints_for(thesis))
    assert "账户" in missing["tp.tiered"]


def test_derive_creates_six_and_attaches_them_to_the_thesis(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    report = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    assert len(report.created) == 6 and report.skipped == ()
    stored = rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)
    assert len(stored) == 6
    assert {row.target_type for row in stored} == {"thesis"}
    assert {row.account_id for row in stored} == {"acc-1"}
    # 写进去的是比例串，库里读出来的是校验过的列表（`tp.tiered` 求值时按这个形状取）
    tier = next(row for row in stored if row.kind == "tp.tiered")
    assert tier.params["tiers"] == [0.5, 1.0]


def test_deriving_twice_creates_nothing(rules, theses) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    deriver = RuleDeriver(rules, theses)
    deriver.derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    again = deriver.derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1")
    assert again.created == ()
    assert len(again.already) == 6
    assert len(rules.by_thesis(thesis.id)) == 6


def test_deriving_an_empty_thesis_creates_only_the_two_that_need_no_level(
    theses, rules
) -> None:
    """一条只有方向和风格的论：价位类全跳过，日历与漂移那两条照样建。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(
        theses,
        entry_low=None,
        entry_high=None,
        stop_price=None,
        target_price=None,
        reward_risk=None,
    )
    report = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id)
    assert {row.kind for row in report.created} == {
        "scheduled.reevaluate",
        "thesis.drift",
    }
    assert len(report.skipped) == 4


def test_a_derived_rule_cannot_be_removed_but_can_be_disabled(theses, rules) -> None:
    """§9.2 那句「不可删除，只能停用」：派生出来的止损提醒不能被一次 `rule remove` 悄悄关掉。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    rule = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created[0]
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不能删除"):
        rules.remove(rule.id, user_id=USER)
    assert rules.set_enabled(rule.id, False, user_id=USER).enabled is False


# --- 改参数 → 回写论上那一列 -------------------------------------------------------------


def test_editing_a_derived_stop_moves_the_thesis_stop(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = next(row for row in made if row.kind == "stop.fixed")
    assert write_back(stop, {"price": 28.0}, {"price": 30.0}) == {"stop_price": 30.0}


def test_editing_a_derived_entry_band_moves_the_upper_edge(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    cross = next(row for row in made if row.kind == "price.cross_below")
    assert write_back(cross, {"price": 36.0}, {"price": 34.0}) == {"entry_high": 34.0}


def test_an_unchanged_number_writes_nothing(theses, rules) -> None:
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id).created
    stop = next(row for row in made if row.kind == "stop.fixed")
    assert write_back(stop, {"price": 28.0}, {"price": 28.0}) == {}


def test_a_rule_that_is_not_derived_writes_back_nothing(theses, rules) -> None:
    """挂在自己心里的价上的一条规则，改了它不该动任何论点。"""
    mine = rules.create(
        USER,
        kind="stop.fixed",
        name="我自己的止损价",
        target_type="symbol",
        target_ref="A/601088",
        params={"price": 20.0},
    )
    assert mine.thesis_id is None
    assert write_back(mine, {"price": 20.0}, {"price": 18.0}) == {}


def test_editing_the_tiers_writes_back_no_target(theses, rules) -> None:
    """分档是比例不是价位：把 `0.5,1.0` 改成 `0.3,0.8` 不该动论上的 `target_price`。"""
    thesis = make_thesis(theses)
    made = RuleDeriver(rules, theses).derive(USER, thesis.id, account_id="acc-1").created
    tier = next(row for row in made if row.kind == "tp.tiered")
    assert write_back(tier, {"tiers": [0.5, 1.0]}, {"tiers": [0.3, 0.8]}) == {}
```

- [ ] **Step 2：跑红**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/services/test_rules_derive.py -q`
Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.derive'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/derive.py`

```python
"""§9.2 建仓派生：一条论 → 六条规则，加那张「改了参数回写哪一列」的表。

派生出来的规则**都挂在论上**（`target_type='thesis'`、`thesis_id` 非空）。这一个形状
决定了两件事：`RuleRepo.remove` 会拒删它们（§9.2「不可删除，只能停用」），而 `write_back`
只在这种规则上生效——用户自己建的一条 `price.cross_below` 里那个数是他心里的价，
不是他论点上的区间上沿，回写会把一条与他当前论点无关的东西写进他的论点。

只有两条规则抄了论上的数字（止损线、买区上沿），所以回写表只有两项。剩下四条读的是
论本身（`tp.tiered` / `thesis.odds_below` / `thesis.drift` 走 `Ask.thesis`，
`scheduled.reevaluate` 走披露日），改了论不必重派它们。

派生不写 `thesis_events`：`to_status` 非空，而派生不是一次状态跳转（`set_levels` 的注释
同一个理由）。它写的是 `rules` 那几行与 `rule_revisions` 的第一份快照。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass

from sams.storage.repos.rules import Rule, RuleRepo
from sams.storage.repos.theses import Thesis, ThesisRepo

#: §9.2 那一行点名的默认阈值：赔率跌破它，这笔就值得重看一遍
ODDS_FLOOR = 1.5
#: 披露日之后几个自然日内提醒复检（§9.3 的周复检节奏里一周的工作日数）
REEVAL_DAYS = 5
#: 分批止盈的两档：到半程与到目标（§10.2「分批提醒」的那个「分批」）。
#: 进 `params` 时用串的形状：模板表把这一族定成 `shape="tiers"`，`_as_tiers` 拒收数字，
#: 而库里存的、求值时读到的是它校验出来的 `list[float]`。
TIERS = "0.5,1.0"

#: 抄过论上数字的那两种参数名（回写只认这一个键）
WRITE_KEY = "price"

#: 改了这条规则的参数 ⇒ 回写论上的哪一列
WRITE_BACK = {"stop.fixed": "stop_price", "price.cross_below": "entry_high"}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Blueprint:
    """一条该建的规则（还没进库）。`reason` 是屏幕上那句「凭什么建这条」。"""

    kind: str
    name: str
    params: dict
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NotDerived:
    """这一条这次不建，并且说得出为什么——沉默的跳过就是「规则怎么少了两条」。"""

    kind: str
    reason: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeriveReport:
    thesis_id: str
    created: tuple[Rule, ...] = ()
    already: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    skipped: tuple[NotDerived, ...] = ()


def blueprints_for(
    thesis: Thesis, *, account_id: str | None = None
) -> tuple[Blueprint | NotDerived, ...]:
    """论上的哪一列派生出哪条规则，一次列全。缺列就是一条 `NotDerived`，不是静默少一条。"""
    name = thesis.code
    out: list[Blueprint | NotDerived] = []
    if thesis.entry_high is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("price.cross_below", "论上没有区间上沿：没有可盯的那条线"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "price.cross_below",
                f"{name} 跌进买入区间",
                {WRITE_KEY: thesis.entry_high},
                f"区间上沿 {thesis.entry_high:g}：从上方跌进来就是可执行计划",
            )
        )
    if thesis.stop_price is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("stop.fixed", "论上没有止损价：三级提醒没有那条线"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "stop.fixed",
                f"{name} 止损三级",
                {WRITE_KEY: thesis.stop_price},
                f"止损价 {thesis.stop_price:g}：pre / hit / urgent 三档由模板给",
            )
        )
    if thesis.target_price is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("tp.tiered", "论上没有目标价：分档没有终点"))
    elif account_id is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("tp.tiered", "没有账户：分批止盈要读这一笔的成本"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "tp.tiered",
                f"{name} 分批止盈",
                {"tiers": TIERS},
                f"目标价 {thesis.target_price:g}：到半程与到终点各提醒一次",
            )
        )
    if thesis.reward_risk is None:
        out.append(NotDerived("thesis.odds_below", "论上没有赔率：这条永远不会成立"))
    else:
        out.append(
            Blueprint(
                "thesis.odds_below",
                f"{name} 赔率跌破阈值",
                {"threshold": ODDS_FLOOR},
                f"现赔率 {thesis.reward_risk:g}：跌破 {ODDS_FLOOR:g} 就值得重看这笔",
            )
        )
    out.append(
        Blueprint(
            "scheduled.reevaluate",
            f"{name} 披露日复检",
            {"within_days": REEVAL_DAYS},
            "新一期财报落地后，论上的数就是旧数",
        )
    )
    out.append(
        Blueprint(
            "thesis.drift",
            f"{name} 观点漂移",
            {},
            "周复检掉一档就提醒（§9.3：只提示，不替谁决定平仓）",
        )
    )
    return tuple(out)


class RuleDeriver:
    """把蓝图落到库里。幂等：同一 (论, kind) 已存在就不再建。"""

    def __init__(self, rules: RuleRepo, theses: ThesisRepo) -> None:
        self.rules = rules
        self.theses = theses

    def derive(
        self, user_id: str, thesis_id: str, *, account_id: str | None = None
    ) -> DeriveReport:
        thesis = self.theses.require(user_id, thesis_id)
        have = {row.kind for row in self.rules.by_thesis(thesis_id)}
        created: list[Rule] = []
        already: list[str] = []
        skipped: list[NotDerived] = []
        for item in blueprints_for(thesis, account_id=account_id):
            if isinstance(item, NotDerived):
                skipped.append(item)
                continue
            if item.kind in have:
                already.append(item.kind)
                continue
            created.append(
                self.rules.create(
                    user_id,
                    kind=item.kind,
                    name=item.name,
                    target_type="thesis",
                    target_ref=thesis_id,
                    params=item.params,
                    account_id=account_id,
                    thesis_id=thesis_id,
                )
            )
            have.add(item.kind)
        return DeriveReport(
            thesis_id=thesis_id,
            created=tuple(created),
            already=tuple(already),
            skipped=tuple(skipped),
        )


def write_back(
    rule: Rule, before: Mapping[str, object], after: Mapping[str, object]
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """编辑一条派生规则时要回写论上的哪几列。空字典 = 不动任何论点（两种情况都常见）。"""
    column = WRITE_BACK.get(rule.kind)
    if column is None or rule.thesis_id is None:
        return {}
    value = after.get(WRITE_KEY)
    if value is None or before.get(WRITE_KEY) == value:
        return {}
    return {column: float(value)}
```

### T-16：`recheck.py`——§9.3 周复检：建基线、比基线，以及那两句不许说的谎

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sams/services/rules/recheck.py`
- Test: `tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py`

**这一枚只做数据侧，投递那一路仍然只有引擎一个出口。三个理由写在这：**

1. **判据只有一份。** 掉档的判定是 T-11 的 `thesis_drift`，复检调用的是同一个函数、同一个
   `Ask`，不重写第二份循环。两份「掉一档」的定义早晚分叉，而分叉的症状是每周复检说没掉、
   规则说掉了，用户不知道信哪个。
2. **复检只发事件，不发消息。** `ThesisDrifted` 是一条记录；「观点漂移，请复核」那句话由
   §9.2 派生出来的 `thesis.drift` 规则在 `rule check` 那一趟命中后投出去（走冷却、走过滤、
   走 D95 那个端口）。两边都投 = 同一件事每周响两遍，而防刷屏是这套系统的生死线（§10.1）。
3. **没有基线的时候必须建，而不是比。** 基线是「建仓时那一眼」，而那一眼在 M2-D 之前没人
   存过（0016 那三列是空的）。第一次跑复检若不写基线，`thesis.drift` 就永远停在「还没有
   基线」上，那条派生规则从此不响——屏幕上它是启用的。

**D96 那两句话在这个文件里各有一枚用例钉着：**「从没比过」不等于「没漂移」（没基线就
不比，只建），反过来「这一趟没算出分」也不等于「没掉档」（有基线但读不出分，那句谎同样
不许说）。合成分被拒的唯一理由是覆盖率不到地板（D70），那是数据问题，不是观点没变。

命令行出口在 T-18 的 `sams rule recheck`；这一枚交付的是服务与那条事件。

- [ ] **Step 1：写失败的测试** —— `tests/services/test_rules_recheck.py`

```python
"""§9.3：opened/tracking 的论每周重算一次，第一次只建基线，之后才比。

用例对着 `support_valuation` 那个世界跑（300 天日线 + 两期年报 + 逐日估值，时钟冻在
`DAYS[-1]`），因为「这一趟到底有没有分」是那条链的回答，不是这个文件的回答。基线用
`set_baseline` 手写到已知档位上，掉档的那几项因此可以逐字断言。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, date, datetime

import pytest
import support_valuation

from sams.core.errors import StorageError, ValidationError
from sams.core.events import EventBus, ThesisDrifted
from sams.services.rules.recheck import (
    BASELINE,
    CLEAN,
    DRIFT,
    NO_SCORE,
    PARTIAL,
    Rechecker,
)
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import RuleData
from sams.storage.repos.theses import OPENED, PROPOSED, TRACKING

CODE = support_valuation.CODE
AS_OF = support_valuation.DAYS[-1]
USER = "u-recheck"
T0 = datetime(2024, 10, 26, 15, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
LEVELS = {
    "entry_low": 30.0,
    "entry_high": 36.0,
    "stop_price": 28.0,
    "target_price": 52.0,
    "reward_risk": 2.0,
}


class Recorder:
    """订阅 `ThesisDrifted`：这一枚文件的验收线有一半在「事件发没发」上。"""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events: list[ThesisDrifted] = []

    def __call__(self, event: ThesisDrifted) -> None:
        self.events.append(event)


@pytest.fixture
def container(db, settings):
    return support_valuation.make_container(db, settings)


def build(container, bus=None) -> Rechecker:
    data = RuleData(
        valuation=container.valuation,
        analysis=container.analysis,
        bars=container.bars,
        symbols=container.symbols,
        accounts=container.sim_accounts,
        ledger=container.sim_ledger,
        theses=container.theses_store,
        watchlists=container.watchlists,
        settings=container.settings,
    )
    return Rechecker(
        theses=container.theses_store,
        data=data,
        bus=bus or EventBus(),
        clock=container.clock,
    )


def open_thesis(container, *, status: str = OPENED, code: str = CODE):
    theses = container.theses_store
    thesis = theses.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code=code,
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
        **LEVELS,
    )
    theses.advance(USER, thesis.id, OPENED, actor_id=USER, now=T0)
    if status == TRACKING:
        theses.advance(USER, thesis.id, TRACKING, actor_id=USER, now=T0)
    return theses.get(USER, thesis.id)


def lay_baseline(container, thesis, items, score=95.0) -> None:
    """把基线写成已知的那几档（`on` 故意写成一个旧日子，好让屏幕能分辨新旧）。"""
    container.theses_store.set_baseline(
        USER, thesis.id, score=score, items=items, on=date(2024, 7, 1), now=T0
    )


# --- 第一次跑：建基线，不比 ----------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_first_run_lays_a_baseline_instead_of_judging(container) -> None:
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [BASELINE]
    row = report.rows[0]
    assert row.score is not None  # 那个世界的合成分确实出得来
    assert row.baseline_score == row.score
    assert "下一次" in row.message
    # 基线落库了，而且属于复检那一天
    stored = container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)
    assert stored.baseline_score == row.score
    assert stored.baseline_on == AS_OF


def test_a_second_run_compares_instead_of_reevaluating_the_baseline(container) -> None:
    """基线已经在了：第二趟必须走比较那一路，不许把基线刷成「今天」。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    rechecker = build(container)
    rechecker.run(USER)
    first = container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)

    again = rechecker.run(USER)
    stored = container.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id)

    assert [row.state for row in again.rows] == [CLEAN]
    assert stored.baseline_on == first.baseline_on


def test_a_thesis_without_a_score_gets_no_baseline_and_says_so(db, settings) -> None:
    """拔掉基本面 → 覆盖不到地板 → 拒分（D70）。这时既不能建基线，也不能说「没漂移」。"""
    thin = support_valuation.make_container(db, settings, with_fundamentals=False)
    thesis = open_thesis(thin)
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(thin, bus).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [NO_SCORE]
    assert report.rows[0].score is None
    assert "覆盖" in report.rows[0].message
    assert thin.theses_store.get(USER, thesis.id).baseline_score is None
    assert rec.events == []


def test_dry_run_writes_nothing_but_still_says_what_it_would_do(container) -> None:
    open_thesis(container)
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER, dry_run=True)

    assert report.dry_run is True
    assert report.rows[0].state == BASELINE
    assert report.rows[0].written is False
    assert "试运行" in report.rows[0].message
    assert report.count(BASELINE) == 1
    # 屏幕上说的是「会建」，库里那三列一个字没动
    assert container.theses_store.list(USER)[0].baseline_score is None
    assert rec.events == []


# --- 之后每次：比基线 ---------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_a_drop_of_one_tier_publishes_the_event_and_names_the_item(container) -> None:
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"pr": 80.0, "roe": 100.0})
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [DRIFT]
    assert report.drifted[0].thesis_id == thesis.id
    assert "市赚率 PR" in report.rows[0].message
    assert "净资产收益率" not in report.rows[0].message  # 没掉的那一项不进这句话
    assert [event.thesis_id for event in rec.events] == [thesis.id]
    assert "复核" in rec.events[0].detail
    assert "只提示" in rec.events[0].detail  # §9.3：系统不替谁决定平仓


def test_a_drop_inside_one_tier_reads_back_as_compared_and_clean(container) -> None:
    """40 → 58 在同一档里（20 分一档，D 那一头的理由）：跨档才算变了。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"pr": 40.0})
    rec = Recorder()
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.subscribe(ThesisDrifted, rec)

    report = build(container, bus).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [CLEAN]
    assert rec.events == []


def test_an_item_the_run_did_not_compute_is_named_not_skipped(container) -> None:
    """基线里有主力净流入、这一趟那一路没数：这句必须是「比不全」，不是「没掉档」。"""
    thesis = open_thesis(container)
    lay_baseline(container, thesis, {"main_inflow": 90.0})

    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert [row.state for row in report.rows] == [PARTIAL]
    assert "主力净流入" in report.rows[0].message
    assert "没掉档" not in report.rows[0].message


def test_a_thesis_with_no_baseline_is_never_reported_as_not_drifted(container) -> None:
    """D96 的那句谎单独钉一次：没有基线时屏幕上出现的那个词只能是「建立基线」。"""
    open_thesis(container)
    report = build(container).run(USER)

    for row in report.rows:
        assert row.state != CLEAN
        assert "未漂移" not in row.message


# --- 谁在复检范围内 -----------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_recheck_walks_opened_and_tracking_only(container) -> None:
    theses = container.theses_store
    open_thesis(container, status=TRACKING)
    idle = theses.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="600000",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
    )

    report = build(container).run(USER)

    assert [row.status for row in report.rows] == [TRACKING]
    assert theses.get(USER, idle.id).baseline_score is None


def test_a_named_thesis_is_rechecked_alone(container) -> None:
    first = open_thesis(container)
    # 同一个代码换一种风格：`(user, market, code, style)` 不同才允许两条同时活着
    second = container.theses_store.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code=CODE,
        style="trend",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
        **LEVELS,
    )
    container.theses_store.advance(USER, second.id, OPENED, actor_id=USER, now=T0)

    report = build(container).run(USER, thesis_id=second.id)

    assert [row.thesis_id for row in report.rows] == [second.id]
    assert report.rows[0].state == BASELINE
    assert container.theses_store.get(USER, first.id).baseline_score is None


def test_a_named_thesis_outside_the_two_statuses_is_refused_with_a_reason(container) -> None:
    """未建仓的论没有「建仓时那一眼」可比：静默返回零行会被读成复检过了。"""
    idle = container.theses_store.create(
        user_id=USER,
        market="A",
        code="600000",
        style="value",
        actor_id=USER,
        now=T0,
        status=PROPOSED,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="opened"):
        build(container).run(USER, thesis_id=idle.id)


def test_an_unknown_thesis_says_unknown(container) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(StorageError, match="thesis"):
        build(container).run(USER, thesis_id="does-not-exist")


def test_the_default_day_is_the_clock_today(container) -> None:
    report = build(container).run(USER)
    assert report.as_of == container.clock.today()
```

Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sams.services.rules.recheck'`

- [ ] **Step 3：写实现** —— `src/sams/services/rules/recheck.py`

```python
"""§9.3 周复检：给每条 `opened`/`tracking` 的论重算一次分，再决定这一次能不能比。

判据不在这里——掉档的判定是 `templates.thesis_drift`，复检调的是同一个函数、同一个 `Ask`。
两份「掉一档」的定义早晚分叉，而分叉的症状是每周复检说没掉、规则说掉了。

复检**只发事件，不发消息**：`ThesisDrifted` 是一条记录，「观点漂移，请复核」那句话由
§9.2 派生的 `thesis.drift` 规则在 `rule check` 那一趟命中后投出去（走过滤、走冷却、走 D95
那个端口）。两边都投就是同一件事每周响两遍，而防刷屏是这套系统的生死线（§10.1）。

第一次跑必须**建**基线而不是比：基线是「建仓时那一眼」，0016 之前那三列一直是空的。不写
基线，`thesis.drift` 就永远停在「还没有基线」，那条派生规则从此不响，而屏幕上它是启用的。

两句谎都不许说（D96）：没有基线 ≠ 没漂移；这一趟没算出分（覆盖不到地板，D70）也 ≠ 没掉档。
前者这一趟只建、不比；后者这一趟不比、并把「比不了」说出来。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import date

from sams.core.clock import SYSTEM_CLOCK, Clock
from sams.core.errors import ValidationError
from sams.core.events import EventBus, ThesisDrifted
from sams.services.rules.snapshot import RuleData, Snapshot, Target
from sams.services.rules.templates import Ask, thesis_drift
from sams.storage.repos.theses import OPENED, TRACKING, Thesis, ThesisRepo

#: 复检只走这两档：`proposed` 还没有「建仓时那一眼」，`hit_*`/`invalidated` 已经过去了
RECHECK_STATUSES = (OPENED, TRACKING)

BASELINE = "baseline"
DRIFT = "drift"
CLEAN = "clean"
PARTIAL = "partial"
NO_SCORE = "no_score"

#: 屏幕上那一列的词（T-18 的 `rule recheck` 打的是这张表）。五个状态说的都是「这一趟
#: 做了什么」，不是「这条论现在怎么样」——后者是那条论的状态机在回答，这里不越权
STATE_NAMES = {
    BASELINE: "建立基线",
    DRIFT: "观点漂移",
    CLEAN: "比过了",
    PARTIAL: "比不全",
    NO_SCORE: "还没有分",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RecheckRow:
    """一条论的一次复检。`message` 是屏幕上那一行，`state` 是它属于哪一类。"""

    thesis_id: str
    user_id: str
    market: str
    code: str
    name: str
    status: str
    state: str
    #: 复检**之前**库里那条基线（`BASELINE` 那一行写的是这一趟刚落库的那个数）
    baseline_score: float | None
    score: float | None
    message: str
    #: 这一趟到底动没动库（`dry_run` 时 False，屏幕上那句要跟着说「试运行」）
    written: bool = False

    @property
    def drifted(self) -> bool:
        return self.state == DRIFT


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RecheckReport:
    user_id: str
    as_of: date
    dry_run: bool
    rows: tuple[RecheckRow, ...] = ()

    def count(self, state: str) -> int:
        return sum(1 for row in self.rows if row.state == state)

    @property
    def drifted(self) -> tuple[RecheckRow, ...]:
        return tuple(row for row in self.rows if row.state == DRIFT)


class Rechecker:
    """一周一次的维护动作：建基线、比基线、把掉档记成事件。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        theses: ThesisRepo,
        data: RuleData,
        bus: EventBus,
        clock: Clock = SYSTEM_CLOCK,
    ) -> None:
        self.theses = theses
        self.data = data
        self.bus = bus
        self.clock = clock

    def run(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        *,
        as_of: date | None = None,
        dry_run: bool = False,
        thesis_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> RecheckReport:
        day = as_of if as_of is not None else self.clock.today()
        targets = self._select(user_id, thesis_id)
        rows = tuple(self._one(thesis, day, dry_run) for thesis in targets)
        return RecheckReport(user_id=user_id, as_of=day, dry_run=dry_run, rows=rows)

    def _select(self, user_id: str, thesis_id: str | None) -> list[Thesis]:
        if thesis_id is None:
            found = list(self.theses.list(user_id, status=OPENED))
            found += list(self.theses.list(user_id, status=TRACKING))
            return found
        thesis = self.theses.require(user_id, thesis_id)
        if thesis.status not in RECHECK_STATUSES:
            allowed = "/".join(RECHECK_STATUSES)
            raise ValidationError(
                f"{thesis.id} 现在是 {thesis.status}，不在复检范围内（只比 {allowed}）："
                "没建过仓的论没有「建仓时那一眼」可比，静默返回零行会被读成复检过了"
            )
        return [thesis]

    def _one(self, thesis: Thesis, as_of: date, dry_run: bool) -> RecheckRow:
        target = Target(thesis.user_id, thesis.market, thesis.code, None, thesis.id)
        snapshot = self.data.read(target, as_of=as_of)
        if thesis.baseline_score is None:
            return self._lay_baseline(thesis, snapshot, as_of, dry_run)
        return self._compare(thesis, snapshot, dry_run)

    def _lay_baseline(
        self, thesis: Thesis, snapshot: Snapshot, as_of: date, dry_run: bool
    ) -> RecheckRow:
        if snapshot.score is None:
            return _row(
                thesis,
                NO_SCORE,
                "这一趟没合成出分（分项覆盖不到地板，D70）：基线与比较都等它有分再说——"
                "现在写下的「基线」会把一条论钉死在一个永远达不到的数上",
                name=snapshot.name,
                score=None,
            )
        if not dry_run:
            self.theses.set_baseline(
                thesis.user_id,
                thesis.id,
                score=snapshot.score,
                items=dict(snapshot.items),
                on=as_of,
                now=self.clock.now_utc(),
            )
        made = _row(
            thesis,
            BASELINE,
            f"基线建立：{snapshot.score:g} 分（{as_of}，{len(snapshot.items)} 个分项）。"
            "这一趟只记下「当时那一眼」，下一次复检才有得比"
            + ("（试运行：没有写库）" if dry_run else ""),
            name=snapshot.name,
            score=snapshot.score,
            written=not dry_run,
        )
        # 屏幕上那一列要说的是这一趟落库的那个数，不是复检之前那个空的
        return made if dry_run else replace(made, baseline_score=snapshot.score)

    def _compare(self, thesis: Thesis, snapshot: Snapshot, dry_run: bool) -> RecheckRow:
        got = thesis_drift(Ask(snapshot=snapshot, params={}, thesis=thesis))
        if got.hit is not None:
            if not dry_run:
                self.bus.publish(
                    ThesisDrifted(
                        thesis_id=thesis.id,
                        user_id=thesis.user_id,
                        detail=got.hit.why,
                        ts=self.clock.now_utc(),
                    )
                )
            return _row(
                thesis,
                DRIFT,
                got.hit.why + ("（试运行：没有发事件）" if dry_run else ""),
                name=snapshot.name,
                score=snapshot.score,
                written=False,
            )
        if got.why_not:
            return _row(
                thesis,
                PARTIAL,
                got.why_not,
                name=snapshot.name,
                score=snapshot.score,
            )
        now = f"{snapshot.score:g} 分" if snapshot.score is not None else "总分这一趟没出"
        return _row(
            thesis,
            CLEAN,
            f"比过了：基线 {thesis.baseline_score:g} 分，{now}，分项没有掉档",
            name=snapshot.name,
            score=snapshot.score,
        )


def _row(
    thesis: Thesis,
    state: str,
    message: str,
    *,
    name: str,
    score: float | None,
    written: bool = False,
) -> RecheckRow:
    return RecheckRow(
        thesis_id=thesis.id,
        user_id=thesis.user_id,
        market=thesis.market,
        code=thesis.code,
        name=name,
        status=thesis.status,
        state=state,
        baseline_score=thesis.baseline_score,
        score=score,
        message=message,
        written=written,
    )
```
